// Every figure on this page comes from real backend aggregates (GET
// /commercial/dashboard, /commercial/travel-search, /reports/sales,
// /commercial/opportunities, /offers). Nothing is fabricated or
// estimated client-side -- a section with no real data source (e.g. a
// monthly sales goal, which nothing in the backend tracks) is left out
// rather than filled with a placeholder number.

type LoadState =
  | { status: 'loading' }
  | { status: 'error'; message: string }
  | {
      status: 'success';
      summary: DashboardSummary;
      upcomingDeparturesCount: number;
      revenueTrend: SalesReportRow[] | null; // null = 403 (role can't see reports), not an error
      opportunities: CommercialOpportunity[];
      offers: Offer[];
      tasks: CommercialTask[];
    };

const MONTH_LABELS_PT = ['Jan', 'Fev', 'Mar', 'Abr', 'Mai', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Ago', 'Set', 'Out', 'Nov', 'Dez'];

function monthLabel(key: string): string {
  const month = Number(key.slice(5, 7));
  return MONTH_LABELS_PT[month - 1] ?? key;
}

function greeting(): string {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  if (hour < 12) return 'Bom dia';
  if (hour < 18) return 'Boa tarde';
  return 'Boa noite';
}

export function DashboardPage() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<LoadState>({ status: 'loading' });
  const [offerIndex, setOfferIndex] = useState(0);
  const [offerModal, setOfferModal] = useState<Offer | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setState({ status: 'loading' });

    const now = new Date();
    const from = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 11, 1).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const to = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    Promise.all([
      getDashboardSummary(),
      getUpcomingTravel('week'),
      getSalesReportByPeriod(from, to).catch((err: unknown) => {
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 403) return null;
        throw err instanceof Error ? err : new Error('Failed to load sales report');
      }),
      listPipelines()
        .then((pipelines) => pipelines.find((p) => p.active) ?? pipelines[0] ?? null)
        .then((pipeline: Pipeline | null) => (pipeline ? listOpportunities(pipeline.id) : [])),
      listOffers(),
      listTasks({ pending: true }).catch(() => ({ tasks: [] as CommercialTask[], total: 0 })),
    ])
      .then(([summary, travel, revenueTrend, opportunities, offers, taskResult]) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setState({
          status: 'success',
          summary,
          upcomingDeparturesCount: travel.operational.length + travel.commercial.length,
          revenueTrend,
          opportunities: opportunities
            .slice()
            .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.updatedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt).getTime())
            .slice(0, 5),
          offers: offers.filter((o) => o.status === 'ACTIVE'),
          tasks: taskResult.tasks,
        });
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        const message =
          error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : 'Não foi possível carregar o painel.';
        setState({ status: 'error', message });
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  if (state.status === 'loading') {
    return <LoadingState label="Carregando resumo da agência…" />;
  }

  if (state.status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div
        role="alert"
        aria-live="polite"
        className="rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
      >
        {state.message}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const { summary, upcomingDeparturesCount, revenueTrend, opportunities, offers } = state;

  const alerts: Array<{ icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>; text: string; sub: string; to: string }> = [];
  if (summary.overdueFollowUpsCount > 0) {
    alerts.push({
      icon: Clock,
      text: `${summary.overdueFollowUpsCount} follow-up${summary.overdueFollowUpsCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} atrasado${summary.overdueFollowUpsCount > 1 ? 's' : ''}`,
      sub: 'Clientes aguardando retorno',
      to: '/pipeline',
    });
  }
  if (summary.overdueReceivablesCount > 0) {
    alerts.push({
      icon: Receipt,
      text: `${summary.overdueReceivablesCount} recebível${summary.overdueReceivablesCount > 1 ? 'is' : ''} vencido${summary.overdueReceivablesCount > 1 ? 's' : ''}`,
      sub: 'Regularize para evitar impacto no caixa',
      to: '/financial/payables',
    });
  }
  if (summary.pescadorReviewQueueCount > 0) {
    alerts.push({
      icon: Fish,
      text: `${summary.pescadorReviewQueueCount} captura${summary.pescadorReviewQueueCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} do Pescador`,
      sub: 'Aguardando revisão',
      to: '/pescador',
    });
  }
  if (summary.postSalePendingCount > 0) {
    alerts.push({
      icon: PackageCheck,
      text: `${summary.postSalePendingCount} pendência${summary.postSalePendingCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} de pós-venda`,
      sub: 'Checklist de pós-venda incompleto',
      to: '/operations/post-trip',
    });
  }
  if (summary.cancelledBookingsCount > 0) {
    alerts.push({
      icon: XCircle,
      text: `${summary.cancelledBookingsCount} reserva${summary.cancelledBookingsCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} cancelada${summary.cancelledBookingsCount > 1 ? 's' : ''}`,
      sub: 'Revise reembolsos/realocações',
      to: '/bookings',
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Visually-hidden page title: the hero below carries the visual
          heading (a greeting, not a redundant "Painel" label matching
          the sidebar nav item), but the page still needs one real
          heading for screen readers/page orientation. */}
      <h1 className="sr-only">Painel</h1>
      {/* Hero */}
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-(--color-travel-navy) via-slate-800 to-(--color-travel-cyan)/40 p-8 text-white shadow-lg">
        <div className="relative z-10 max-w-xl">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-cyan-200">{greeting()}</p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Que tal transformar mais sonhos em viagens hoje?</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-200">
            {summary.openOpportunitiesCount} oportunidade{summary.openOpportunitiesCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} em andamento ·{' '}
            {summary.proposalsWaitingCount} proposta{summary.proposalsWaitingCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} aguardando resposta
          </p>
          <Link to="/proposals">
            <Button className="mt-5 bg-white text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100">
              Nova proposta
              <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 right-0 hidden w-1/2 opacity-30 [background:radial-gradient(circle_at_70%_50%,rgba(6,182,212,0.6),transparent_60%)] sm:block" />
      </div>

      {/* KPI chips */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <KpiChip
          label="Vendas (mês)"
          value={formatBRL(Number(summary.salesThisMonthTotal))}
          icon={<TrendingUp className="h-5 w-5" />}
          tone="green"
        />
        <KpiChip
          label="Propostas aguardando resposta"
          value={String(summary.proposalsWaitingCount)}
          icon={<FileText className="h-5 w-5" />}
          tone="blue"
        />
        <KpiChip
          label="Viagens futuras"
          value={String(summary.upcomingTripsCount)}
          icon={<Map className="h-5 w-5" />}
          tone="purple"
        />
        <KpiChip
          label="Próximas partidas (7 dias)"
          value={String(upcomingDeparturesCount)}
          icon={<Plane className="h-5 w-5" />}
          tone="blue"
        />
        <KpiChip
          label="Vendas pendentes"
          value={String(summary.pendingSalesCount)}
          icon={<Wallet className="h-5 w-5" />}
          tone="orange"
        />
        <KpiChip
          label="Follow-ups hoje"
          value={String(summary.followUpsDueTodayCount)}
          icon={<CalendarClock className="h-5 w-5" />}
          tone="green"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="space-y-6 lg:col-span-2">
          {revenueTrend && revenueTrend.length > 0 && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Receita da agência</CardTitle>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Evolução das vendas nos últimos 12 meses</p>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
                  <AreaChart data={revenueTrend.map((r) => ({ ...r, monthLabel: monthLabel(r.key) }))}>
                    <defs>
                      <linearGradient id="revenueFill" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                        <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#2563eb" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                        <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#2563eb" stopOpacity={0} />
                      </linearGradient>
                    </defs>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#e2e8f0" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="monthLabel" tick={{ fontSize: 12, fill: '#64748b' }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis
                      tick={{ fontSize: 12, fill: '#64748b' }}
                      axisLine={false}
                      tickLine={false}
                      tickFormatter={(v: number) => (v >= 1000 ? `${Math.round(v / 1000)} mil` : String(v))}
                    />
                    <Tooltip
                      formatter={(value) => formatBRL(Number(value))}
                      labelFormatter={(_label, payload) => (payload?.[0]?.payload as { key?: string } | undefined)?.key ?? ''}
                    />
                    <Area type="monotone" dataKey="total" stroke="#2563eb" strokeWidth={2} fill="url(#revenueFill)" />
                  </AreaChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          <Card>
            <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <CardTitle>Oportunidades em andamento</CardTitle>
              <Link to="/pipeline" className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700">
                Ver todas
              </Link>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              {opportunities.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-400">Nenhuma oportunidade em andamento</p>
              ) : (
                <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {opportunities.map((opp) => (
                    <li key={opp.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-3 first:pt-0 last:pb-0">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">{opp.customerName ?? 'Cliente'}</p>
                        <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{opp.destination ?? 'Destino a definir'}</p>
                      </div>
                      <span className="shrink-0 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {opp.expectedValue !== undefined ? formatBRL(opp.expectedValue) : '—'}
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* Tasks widget */}
          {state.tasks.length > 0 && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <CardTitle>Tarefas pendentes</CardTitle>
                <Link to="/tasks" className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700">
                  Ver todas
                </Link>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {state.tasks.slice(0, 5).map((task) => (
                    <li key={task.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2">
                      <Circle className={`h-4 w-4 shrink-0 ${
                        new Date(task.dueAt) < new Date() ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-slate-300'
                      }`} />
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 truncate">{task.title}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{formatDateBR(task.dueAt)}</p>
                      </div>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          {alerts.length > 0 && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
                  Alertas operacionais
                </CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-1">
                {alerts.map((alert) => (
                  <Link
                    key={alert.text}
                    to={alert.to}
                    className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-md p-2 -mx-2 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50"
                  >
                    <span className="mt-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-50 p-1.5 text-amber-600">
                      <alert.icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </span>
                    <span className="min-w-0">
                      <span className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">{alert.text}</span>
                      <span className="block text-xs text-slate-500">{alert.sub}</span>
                    </span>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Resumo financeiro</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-3">
              <SummaryRow label="Propostas enviadas" value={String(summary.sentProposalsCount)} />
              <SummaryRow label="Propostas aceitas" value={String(summary.acceptedProposalsCount)} />
              <SummaryRow label="Valor em propostas abertas" value={formatBRL(Number(summary.openProposalValueSum))} />
              <SummaryRow label="Vendas confirmadas" value={String(summary.confirmedSalesCount)} />
              <SummaryRow label="Vendas pagas" value={String(summary.paidSalesCount)} />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Offers carousel */}
      {offers.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <CardTitle>Ofertas em destaque</CardTitle>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => setOfferIndex((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1))}
                disabled={offerIndex === 0}
                aria-label="Ofertas anteriores"
              >
                <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => setOfferIndex((i) => Math.min(offers.length - 3, i + 1))}
                disabled={offerIndex >= offers.length - 3}
                aria-label="Próximas ofertas"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="flex gap-4 transition-transform duration-300"
                style={{ transform: `translateX(-${offerIndex * (100 / 3)}%)` }}
              >
                {offers.map((offer, i) => (
                  <div key={offer.id} className="w-full shrink-0 sm:w-[calc(50%-0.5rem)] lg:w-[calc(33.333%-0.667rem)]">
                    <OfferCard offer={offer} gradientIndex={i} onOfferToCustomer={() => setOfferModal(offer)} />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <Link to="/proposals">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
            <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
            Nova proposta
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link to="/customers">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
            <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4" />
            Cadastrar cliente
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link to="/trips">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
            <Map className="h-4 w-4" />
            Nova viagem
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link to="/financial/payments">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
            <Wallet className="h-4 w-4" />
            Adicionar pagamento
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {offerModal && (
        <OfferToCustomerModal offer={offerModal} onClose={() => setOfferModal(null)} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryRow({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
      <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">{label}</span>
      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{value}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

const OFFER_GRADIENTS = [
  'from-cyan-500 to-blue-700',
  'from-violet-500 to-fuchsia-700',
  'from-emerald-500 to-teal-700',
  'from-amber-500 to-orange-700',
  'from-blue-500 to-indigo-700',
  'from-rose-500 to-pink-700',
];

function OfferCard({
  offer,
  gradientIndex,
  onOfferToCustomer,
}: {
  offer: Offer;
  gradientIndex: number;
  onOfferToCustomer: () => void;
}) {
  const gradient = OFFER_GRADIENTS[gradientIndex % OFFER_GRADIENTS.length];
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
      <div className={cn('flex h-28 items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br text-3xl', gradient)}>
        <Plane className="h-9 w-9 text-white/90" />
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 p-4">
        <p className="line-clamp-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{offer.name}</p>
        {offer.description && <p className="line-clamp-2 text-xs text-slate-500">{offer.description}</p>}
        <p className="mt-auto text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{formatBRL(offer.price)}</p>
        {offer.validUntil && (
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Válida até {formatDateBR(offer.validUntil, { assumeDateOnly: true })}</p>
        )}
        <Button size="sm" className="mt-1" onClick={onOfferToCustomer}>
          Ofertar ao cliente
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function OfferToCustomerModal({ offer, onClose }: { offer: Offer; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [customerId, setCustomerId] = useState('');
  const [price, setPrice] = useState(String(offer.price));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [createdId, setCreatedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleSubmit() {
    if (!customerId) {
      setError('Selecione um cliente.');
      return;
    }
    const proposedPrice = Number(price.replace(',', '.'));
    if (!Number.isFinite(proposedPrice) || proposedPrice <= 0) {
      setError('Informe um valor válido.');
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const proposal = await createProposal({ customerId, offerId: offer.id, proposedPrice });
      setCreatedId(proposal.id);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Não foi possível criar a proposta.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={`Ofertar "${offer.name}" a um cliente`}
      footer={
        createdId ? (
          <Link to={`/proposals/${createdId}`}>
            <Button size="sm">Ver proposta</Button>
          </Link>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onClose} disabled={saving}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" onClick={() => void handleSubmit()} disabled={saving}>
              {saving ? 'Criando…' : 'Criar proposta'}
            </Button>
          </>
        )
      }
    >
      {createdId ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Proposta criada com sucesso. Você pode acompanhá-la a partir de agora em Propostas.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-2 text-xs text-red-700">{error}</p>}
          <CustomerCombobox
            value={customerId}
            onSelect={(customer: CustomerLookupItem | null) => setCustomerId(customer?.id ?? '')}
          />
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Valor da proposta</label>
            <Input value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} inputMode="decimal" />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}
/**
 * Dashboard (DASH-01): code catalog of widgets + per-agency layout.
 *
 * The layout is presentation only (order + enabled). Authorization is data:
 * each widget declares `canView`, evaluated by the server on EVERY read for the
 * viewer, and its loader reuses the existing report/summary functions (no second
 * data path). A widget the viewer may not see is omitted entirely -- no key, no
 * title, no "blocked" placeholder. The stored layout never carries filters,
 * queries, permissions or user ids.
 */
import { ForbiddenError, getAgencyId, getTenantContext, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import { requireRoleOrAreaGrant } from './area-grants';
import { assertNotRestricted } from './permission-restrictions';
import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
import { getDashboardSummary } from './commercial-cockpit';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from './database';
import { ConflictError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import { getCashFlowSummary } from './financial';
import {
  getCommissionsProjection,
  getReceivablesProjection,
  getRevenueForecast,
  getSupplierBalances,
  todayCivil,
} from './report-projections-service';

export interface WidgetDefinition {
  key: string;
  title: string;
  /** Returns true if the current viewer may see this widget (same guard as the source endpoint). */
  canView(database: DatabaseRuntime): Promise<boolean>;
  load(database: DatabaseRuntime): Promise<unknown>;
  /** Travel Lite widget key, for future Lite -> Full layout migration; null = no counterpart. */
  liteKey: string | null;
}

const rolePasses = (minRole: UserRole): boolean => {
  try {
    requireRole(minRole);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) return false;
    throw error;
  }
};

const monthRange = () => {
  const today = todayCivil();
  const last = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(today.slice(0, 4)), Number(today.slice(5, 7)), 0)).getUTCDate();
  return { from: new Date(`${today.slice(0, 8)}01T00:00:00.000Z`), to: new Date(`${today.slice(0, 8)}${String(last).padStart(2, '0')}T23:59:59.999Z`) };
};

export const WIDGET_CATALOG: readonly WidgetDefinition[] = [
  {
    key: 'commercial_overview',
    title: 'Visão comercial',
    canView: () => Promise.resolve(rolePasses(UserRole.VIEWER)),
    load: (database) => getDashboardSummary(database, getUserId(), undefined),
    liteKey: 'sales_summary',
  },
  {
    key: 'financial_overview',
    title: 'Fluxo de caixa do mês',
    // Same guard as GET /financial/dashboard (role floor or the FINANCIAL_OVERVIEW area grant).
    canView: async (database) => {
      try {
        await requireRoleOrAreaGrant(database, UserRole.MANAGER, 'FINANCIAL_OVERVIEW');
        const context = getTenantContext();
        await database.withTenantTransaction((client) => assertNotRestricted(client, context.userRole, 'financial', 'view'));
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) return false;
        throw error;
      }
    },
    load: (database) => getCashFlowSummary(database, monthRange()),
    liteKey: 'cash_flow',
  },
  {
    key: 'receivables_projection',
    title: 'Recebíveis de clientes',
    canView: async (database) => {
      try {
        await requireRoleOrAreaGrant(database, UserRole.MANAGER, 'FINANCIAL_RECEIVABLES');
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) return false;
        throw error;
      }
    },
    load: (database) => getReceivablesProjection(database, {}),
    liteKey: null,
  },
  {
    key: 'revenue_forecast',
    title: 'Receita da agência (projeção)',
    canView: () => Promise.resolve(rolePasses(UserRole.MANAGER)),
    load: (database) => getRevenueForecast(database, {}),
    liteKey: null,
  },
  {
    key: 'supplier_balances',
    title: 'Saldos com fornecedores',
    canView: () => Promise.resolve(rolePasses(UserRole.MANAGER)),
    load: (database) => getSupplierBalances(database, {}),
    liteKey: null,
  },
  {
    key: 'commissions_projection',
    title: 'Comissões de funcionários',
    canView: async (database) => {
      if (!rolePasses(UserRole.MANAGER)) return false;
      const context = getTenantContext();
      try {
        await database.withTenantTransaction((client) => assertNotRestricted(client, context.userRole, 'employees', 'view'));
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) return false;
        throw error;
      }
    },
    load: (database) => getCommissionsProjection(database, {}),
    liteKey: null,
  },
];

export interface LayoutEntry {
  key: string;
  enabled: boolean;
}

const defaultLayout = (): LayoutEntry[] => WIDGET_CATALOG.map((widget) => ({ key: widget.key, enabled: true }));

/** Stored layout reconciled with the catalog: unknown keys dropped, missing keys appended enabled. */
function reconcile(stored: LayoutEntry[] | null): LayoutEntry[] {
  const known = new Set(WIDGET_CATALOG.map((widget) => widget.key));
  const kept = (stored ?? []).filter((entry) => known.has(entry.key));
  const present = new Set(kept.map((entry) => entry.key));
  return [...kept, ...WIDGET_CATALOG.filter((widget) => !present.has(widget.key)).map((widget) => ({ key: widget.key, enabled: true }))];
}

async function loadLayout(database: DatabaseRuntime): Promise<{ widgets: LayoutEntry[]; version: number }> {
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<{ widgets: LayoutEntry[]; version: number }>(
      `SELECT widgets, version FROM dashboard_layouts WHERE agency_id = $1`,
      [getAgencyId()],
    );
    const row = result.rows[0];
    return row ? { widgets: reconcile(row.widgets), version: row.version } : { widgets: defaultLayout(), version: 0 };
  });
}

/** The viewer's dashboard: enabled widgets in order, only those the viewer may see, each with its data. */
export async function getViewerDashboard(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
  requireRole(UserRole.VIEWER);
  const layout = await loadLayout(database);
  const byKey = new Map(WIDGET_CATALOG.map((widget) => [widget.key, widget]));
  const widgets: Array<{ key: string; title: string; data: unknown }> = [];
  for (const entry of layout.widgets) {
    if (!entry.enabled) continue;
    const definition = byKey.get(entry.key);
    if (!definition || !(await definition.canView(database))) continue;
    try {
      widgets.push({ key: definition.key, title: definition.title, data: await definition.load(database) });
    } catch (error) {
      // A widget that cannot be computed (e.g. too many rows) must not take the whole dashboard down.
      if (!(error instanceof ValidationError)) throw error;
      widgets.push({ key: definition.key, title: definition.title, data: { unavailable: 'TOO_MANY_ROWS' } });
    }
  }
  return { widgets };
}

/** Administrator view: the whole catalog (metadata only, no data) with enabled flags, order and version. */
export async function getDashboardSettings(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  const layout = await loadLayout(database);
  const byKey = new Map(WIDGET_CATALOG.map((widget) => [widget.key, widget]));
  return {
    version: layout.version,
    widgets: layout.widgets.map((entry) => ({ key: entry.key, title: byKey.get(entry.key)?.title ?? entry.key, enabled: entry.enabled })),
  };
}

export function parseDashboardInput(body: unknown): { version: number; widgets: LayoutEntry[] } {
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
  const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => !['version', 'widgets'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown field in request body');
  if (!Number.isInteger(record.version) || (record.version as number) < 0) throw new ValidationError('Field "version" is invalid');
  if (!Array.isArray(record.widgets)) throw new ValidationError('Field "widgets" must be a list');
  const known = new Set(WIDGET_CATALOG.map((widget) => widget.key));
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const widgets = record.widgets.map((raw: unknown) => {
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid widget entry');
    const entry = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(entry).some((key) => !['key', 'enabled'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Widgets only carry key and enabled');
    if (typeof entry.key !== 'string' || !known.has(entry.key)) throw new ValidationError('Unknown widget');
    if (typeof entry.enabled !== 'boolean') throw new ValidationError('Field "enabled" must be boolean');
    if (seen.has(entry.key)) throw new ValidationError('Duplicate widget');
    seen.add(entry.key);
    return { key: entry.key, enabled: entry.enabled };
  });
  if (seen.size !== known.size) throw new ValidationError('Every catalog widget must be listed exactly once');
  return { version: record.version as number, widgets };
}

export async function saveDashboardSettings(database: DatabaseRuntime, input: { version: number; widgets: LayoutEntry[] }) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  getTenantContext();
  await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const current = await client.query<{ version: number }>(
      `SELECT version FROM dashboard_layouts WHERE agency_id = $1 FOR UPDATE`,
      [getAgencyId()],
    );
    const currentVersion = current.rows[0]?.version ?? 0;
    if (currentVersion !== input.version) throw new ConflictError('The dashboard layout was changed by someone else; reload and try again');
    if (current.rows[0]) {
      await client.query(
        `UPDATE dashboard_layouts SET widgets = $2::jsonb, version = version + 1, updated_by_user_id = $3, updated_at = now() WHERE agency_id = $1`,
        [getAgencyId(), JSON.stringify(input.widgets), getUserId()],
      );
    } else {
      // FOR UPDATE cannot lock a row that does not exist yet: the loser of a first-save race inserts nothing.
      const inserted = await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO dashboard_layouts (agency_id, widgets, version, updated_by_user_id) VALUES ($1, $2::jsonb, 1, $3)
         ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO NOTHING`,
        [getAgencyId(), JSON.stringify(input.widgets), getUserId()],
      );
      if (inserted.rowCount === 0) throw new ConflictError('The dashboard layout was changed by someone else; reload and try again');
    }
    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.DASHBOARD_CONFIGURED,
      entityType: 'dashboard_layout',
      required: true,
      metadata: { fieldsChanged: input.widgets.filter((entry) => entry.enabled).map((entry) => entry.key), toStatus: `v${currentVersion + 1}` },
    });
  });
  return getDashboardSettings(database);
}

import { requireRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/types';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../database';
import { isCsvRequest, sendReportCsv } from '../report-csv';
import {
  getCommissionsProjection,
  getReceivablesProjection,
  getRevenueForecast,
  getSupplierBalances,
  parseProjectionFilters,
} from '../report-projections-service';

type Query = Record<string, unknown>;

/**
 * Projection reports (Frente 9). MANAGER+ like the other management reports;
 * the sale filters are the same in every report and the CSV goes through the
 * existing report-csv contract (formula protection, ceilings).
 */
export function registerReportProjectionRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: { database: DatabaseRuntime; protectedHooks: preHandlerHooks },
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get<{ Querystring: Query }>('/reports/receivables-projection', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    const csv = isCsvRequest(request.query.format);
    const result = await getReceivablesProjection(database, parseProjectionFilters(request.query));
    if (csv) {
      return sendReportCsv(reply, 'recebiveis-projecao.csv', ['period', 'openBalance', 'count'], [
        ['late', result.late.openBalance, result.late.count],
        ...result.byMonth.map((row) => [row.month, row.openBalance, row.count]),
      ]);
    }
    return result;
  });

  app.get<{ Querystring: Query }>('/reports/revenue-forecast', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    const csv = isCsvRequest(request.query.format);
    const result = await getRevenueForecast(database, parseProjectionFilters(request.query, true));
    if (csv) {
      return sendReportCsv(reply, 'receita-projecao.csv', ['supplierId', 'contracted', 'projected', 'undefined'],
        result.bySupplier.map((row) => [row.supplierId, row.contracted, row.projected, row.undefined]));
    }
    return result;
  });

  app.get<{ Querystring: Query }>('/reports/supplier-balances', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    const csv = isCsvRequest(request.query.format);
    const rows = await getSupplierBalances(database, parseProjectionFilters(request.query, true));
    if (csv) {
      return sendReportCsv(reply, 'saldos-fornecedores.csv', ['supplierId', 'expected', 'received', 'difference'],
        rows.map((row) => [row.supplierId, row.expected, row.received, row.difference]));
    }
    return { rows };
  });

  app.get<{ Querystring: Query }>('/reports/commissions-projection', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    const csv = isCsvRequest(request.query.format);
    const result = await getCommissionsProjection(database, parseProjectionFilters(request.query));
    if (csv) {
      return sendReportCsv(reply, 'comissoes-projecao.csv', ['forecast', 'released', 'paid', 'releasedNotPaid', 'notYetReleased'],
        [[result.forecast, result.released, result.paid, result.releasedNotPaid, result.notYetReleased]]);
    }
    return result;
  });
}

import type { FastifyInstance, preHandlerHookHandler } from 'fastify';
import { requireRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/types';
import {
  calculateRevenueForSale,
  cancelRevenueEntry,
  createRuleVersion,
  getSaleRevenue,
  listRuleVersions,
  listRevenueEntries,
  parseRuleInput,
  receiveRevenueEntry,
  reactivateRevenueEntry,
} from '../agency-revenue';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../database';
import { ValidationError } from '../errors';

export function registerAgencyRevenueRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: { database: DatabaseRuntime; protectedHooks: preHandlerHookHandler[] },
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get('/agency-revenue/rules', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async () => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return { rules: await listRuleVersions(database) };
  });

  app.get('/agency-revenue/entries', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    const query = request.query as Record<string, unknown>;
    const allowed = new Set(['saleId', 'status', 'supplierId', 'categoryId']);
    if (Object.keys(query).some((key) => !allowed.has(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown revenue entry filter');
    const optional = (value: unknown, field: string): string | undefined => {
      if (value === undefined) return undefined;
      if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.trim().length < 1 || value.length > 100) throw new ValidationError(`Invalid ${field}`);
      return value.trim();
    };
    const status = optional(query.status, 'status');
    if (status !== undefined && !['PENDING_RULE','FORECAST','ENTITLED','AVAILABLE','RECEIVED','CANCELLED'].includes(status)) {
      throw new ValidationError('Invalid revenue entry status');
    }
    const filters = {
      ...(optional(query.saleId,'saleId') ? { saleId: optional(query.saleId,'saleId')! } : {}),
      ...(status ? { status: status as 'PENDING_RULE'|'FORECAST'|'ENTITLED'|'AVAILABLE'|'RECEIVED'|'CANCELLED' } : {}),
      ...(optional(query.supplierId,'supplierId') ? { supplierId: optional(query.supplierId,'supplierId')! } : {}),
      ...(optional(query.categoryId,'categoryId') ? { categoryId: optional(query.categoryId,'categoryId')! } : {}),
    };
    return { entries: await listRevenueEntries(database, filters), limit: 500 };
  });

  app.post('/agency-revenue/rules', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const created = await createRuleVersion(database, parseRuleInput(request.body));
    reply.code(201);
    return { rule: created };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/sales/:id/agency-revenue', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return getSaleRevenue(database, request.params.id);
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/sales/:id/agency-revenue/calculate', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return calculateRevenueForSale(database, request.params.id);
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/receive', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = request.body;
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
    const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => !['amount', 'reference'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown field in request body');
    if (typeof record.amount !== 'number') throw new ValidationError('Field "amount" must be a number');
    if (record.reference !== undefined && (typeof record.reference !== 'string' || record.reference.length > 200)) {
      throw new ValidationError('Field "reference" is invalid');
    }
    return receiveRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id, {
      amount: record.amount,
      reference: typeof record.reference === 'string' ? record.reference : null,
    });
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/cancel', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    return cancelRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id);
  });
  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/reactivate', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = request.body;
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
    const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => key !== 'reason') || typeof record.reason !== 'string') {
      throw new ValidationError('A reason is required');
    }
    return reactivateRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id, record.reason);
  });
}
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { PageHeader } from '../components/layout/PageHeader';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '../components/ui/card';
import { api } from '../lib/api';

interface Receivables {
  late: { count: number; openBalance: number };
  byMonth: Array<{ month: string; openBalance: number; count: number }>;
  totalOpen: number;
}
interface Revenue {
  contracted: { total: number };
  projected: { total: number; byMonth: Array<{ month: string; amount: number }> };
  undefined: { total: number };
  grandTotal: number;
}
interface SupplierBalance {
  supplierId: string | null;
  expected: number;
  received: number;
  difference: number;
}
interface Commissions {
  forecast: number;
  released: number;
  paid: number;
  releasedNotPaid: number;
  notYetReleased: number;
}

const money = (value: number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(value);

const FILTERS = [
  ['saleFrom', 'Venda de', 'date'],
  ['saleTo', 'Venda até', 'date'],
  ['travelFrom', 'Embarque de', 'date'],
  ['travelTo', 'Embarque até', 'date'],
  ['origin', 'Origem', 'text'],
  ['destination', 'Destino', 'text'],
] as const;

/**
 * Projections (Frente 9). The server computes everything for the session's
 * agency; this page only sends the filters and shows the four reports apart:
 * customer receivables, agency revenue, supplier balances and employee
 * commission are never added together.
 */
export function ProjectionsPage() {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<Receivables | null>(null);
  const [revenue, setRevenue] = useState<Revenue | null>(null);
  const [balances, setBalances] = useState<SupplierBalance[]>([]);
  const [commissions, setCommissions] = useState<Commissions | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [exportError, setExportError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const query = Object.entries(filters)
    .filter(([, value]) => value !== '')
    .map(([key, value]) => `${key}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}`)
    .join('&');
  const withQuery = (path: string) => (query ? `${path}?${query}` : path);

  const latest = useRef(0);
  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    // Only the newest request may write the state (a slower, older response is dropped).
    const mine = ++latest.current;
    setError(null);
    try {
      const [r, v, b, c] = await Promise.all([
        api.get<Receivables>(withQuery('/reports/receivables-projection')),
        api.get<Revenue>(withQuery('/reports/revenue-forecast')),
        api.get<{ rows: SupplierBalance[] }>(withQuery('/reports/supplier-balances')),
        api.get<Commissions>(withQuery('/reports/commissions-projection')),
      ]);
      if (mine !== latest.current) return;
      setReceivables(r.data);
      setRevenue(v.data);
      setBalances(b.data.rows);
      setCommissions(c.data);
    } catch (err) {
      if (mine !== latest.current) return;
      // Never leave numbers of the previous filters under the new ones.
      setReceivables(null);
      setRevenue(null);
      setBalances([]);
      setCommissions(null);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Não foi possível carregar as projeções.');
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps -- reload only when the filters are applied
  }, [query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [load]);

  async function exportCsv(path: string) {
    setExportError(null);
    try {
      const { blob, filename } = await api.downloadCsv(withQuery(path));
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (err) {
      setExportError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Não foi possível exportar.');
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <PageHeader title="Projeções" />
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Filtros</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          {FILTERS.map(([key, label, type]) => (
            <div key={key}>
              <label htmlFor={`proj-${key}`} className="block text-xs text-slate-500">
                {label}
              </label>
              <input
                id={`proj-${key}`}
                type={type}
                value={filters[key] ?? ''}
                onChange={(event) => setFilters({ ...filters, [key]: event.target.value })}
                className="mt-1 rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1 text-sm"
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-700">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      {exportError && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-700">
          {exportError}
        </p>
      )}

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Recebíveis de clientes</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {receivables && (
            <>
              <p>Em atraso: {money(receivables.late.openBalance)} ({receivables.late.count})</p>
              {receivables.byMonth.map((row) => (
                <p key={row.month}>
                  {row.month}: {money(row.openBalance)}
                </p>
              ))}
              <p>Total em aberto: {money(receivables.totalOpen)}</p>
            </>
          )}
          <button type="button" className="rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1" onClick={() => void exportCsv('/reports/receivables-projection')}>
            Exportar CSV (recebíveis)
          </button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Receita da agência</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {revenue && (
            <>
              <p>Contratada: {money(revenue.contracted.total)}</p>
              <p>Projetada: {money(revenue.projected.total)}</p>
              <p>Sem data definida: {money(revenue.undefined.total)}</p>
            </>
          )}
          <button type="button" className="rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1" onClick={() => void exportCsv('/reports/revenue-forecast')}>
            Exportar CSV (receita)
          </button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Saldos com fornecedores</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {balances.map((row) => (
            <p key={row.supplierId ?? 'none'}>
              {row.supplierId ?? 'Sem fornecedor'}: esperado {money(row.expected)} · recebido {money(row.received)} · diferença{' '}
              {money(row.difference)}
            </p>
          ))}
          <button type="button" className="rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1" onClick={() => void exportCsv('/reports/supplier-balances')}>
            Exportar CSV (fornecedores)
          </button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Comissões de funcionários</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
          {commissions && (
            <>
              <p>Prevista: {money(commissions.forecast)}</p>
              <p>Liberada: {money(commissions.released)}</p>
              <p>Paga: {money(commissions.paid)}</p>
              <p>Liberada e não paga: {money(commissions.releasedNotPaid)}</p>
            </>
          )}
          <button type="button" className="rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1" onClick={() => void exportCsv('/reports/commissions-projection')}>
            Exportar CSV (comissões)
          </button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

18:// DashboardSummary shape returned by GET /commercial/dashboard
154:// see services/api/src/commercial-cockpit.ts:getDashboardSummary.

178:export async function getDashboardSummary(): Promise<DashboardSummary> {

784:  dashboard: FinancialDashboardMetrics;
787:export interface FinancialDashboardMetrics {
/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-member-access, @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-assignment, @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any, @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-return, @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-call -- HTTP/JSON and pg row fixtures in this integration test are intentionally untyped */
import { readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { Pool } from 'pg';

// Own container/port: never the shared Onda B database, the demo or 55438.
vi.hoisted(() => {
  process.env.ONDA_B_TEST_DB_PORT ??= '55631';
  process.env.ONDA_B_TEST_DB_CONTAINER ??= 'travel-full-proj-int';
  process.env.ONDA_B_TEST_DB_PROJECT ??= 'travel-full-proj-int';
});

import { buildApp } from '../../../services/api/src/app';
import type { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import type { AuthenticatedPrincipal } from '../../../services/api/src/auth';
import { createDatabaseRuntime } from '../../../services/api/src/database';
import {
  startDisposableDb,
  type DisposableDb,
} from '../../../services/api/tests/helpers/disposable-postgres';

const MIGRATIONS = readdirSync(resolve(import.meta.dirname, '../../../infrastructure/migrations'))
  .filter((name) => /^\d+_.+\.sql$/.test(name))
  .sort();

const agencyA = '10000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a9';
const agencyB = '20000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000b9';
const ids = {
  adminA: '11000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a2',
  managerA: '11000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a3',
  agentA: '11000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a4',
  adminB: '21000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000b1',
};
const roleOf: Record<keyof typeof ids, [string, UserRole]> = {
  adminA: [agencyA, 'ADMIN' as UserRole],
  managerA: [agencyA, 'MANAGER' as UserRole],
  agentA: [agencyA, 'AGENT' as UserRole],
  adminB: [agencyB, 'ADMIN' as UserRole],
};
const civil = (offsetDays: number) =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo' }).format(new Date(Date.now() + offsetDays * 86400000));

describe('Report projections (Frente 9) over HTTP with a real database', () => {
  let db: DisposableDb;
  let admin: Pool;
  let supplier: string;
  let customerA: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await startDisposableDb(MIGRATIONS);
    admin = db.adminPool;
    await admin.query(
      `INSERT INTO agencies (id, name, slug, email, plan, status) VALUES
        ($1, 'Agency A', 'agency-a-proj', 'a@example.test', 'FREE', 'ACTIVE'),
        ($2, 'Agency B', 'agency-b-proj', 'b@example.test', 'FREE', 'ACTIVE')`,
      [agencyA, agencyB],
    );
    for (const [key, id] of Object.entries(ids)) {
      const [agencyId, role] = roleOf[key as keyof typeof ids];
      await admin.query(
        `INSERT INTO users (id, agency_id, email, name, role, password_hash, status)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'hash-test-only', 'ACTIVE')`,
        [id, agencyId, `${key}@example.test`, key, role],
      );
    }
    supplier = (await admin.query<{ id: string }>(`INSERT INTO suppliers (agency_id, name) VALUES ($1, '=Fornecedor') RETURNING id`, [agencyA])).rows[0]!.id;
    customerA = (await admin.query<{ id: string }>(`INSERT INTO customers (agency_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Cliente', 'ACTIVE') RETURNING id`, [agencyA])).rows[0]!.id;
  }, 300_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await db?.stop();
  });

  function app() {
    return buildApp({
      authProvider: {
        async authenticate(request) {
          const token = request.headers['x-test-user'];
          if (typeof token !== 'string') return null;
          const row = (await admin.query<{ id: string; agency_id: string; role: string; email: string }>(
            `SELECT id, agency_id, role, email FROM users WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'ACTIVE'`, [token])).rows[0];
          if (!row) return null;
          const principal: AuthenticatedPrincipal = { userId: row.id, agencyId: row.agency_id, role: row.role as UserRole, email: row.email };
          return principal;
        },
      },
      validateUserAgencyAccess: async (userId, agencyId) =>
        (await admin.query(`SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE id = $1 AND agency_id = $2`, [userId, agencyId])).rowCount === 1,
      database: createDatabaseRuntime(db.runtimePool),
    });
  }

  async function get(as: string | null, url: string) {
    const server = app();
    try {
      const res = await server.inject({ url, ...(as ? { headers: { 'x-test-user': as } } : {}) });
      const type = String(res.headers['content-type'] ?? '');
      return { status: res.statusCode, headers: res.headers, text: res.body, body: type.includes('json') ? res.json<Record<string, any>>() : {} };
    } finally {
      await server.close();
    }
  }
  async function post(as: string, url: string, payload?: unknown) {
    const server = app();
    try {
      const res = await server.inject({ method: 'POST', url, headers: { 'x-test-user': as }, ...(payload !== undefined ? { payload: payload as object } : {}) });
      return { status: res.statusCode, body: res.json<Record<string, any>>() };
    } finally {
      await server.close();
    }
  }

  const reset = async () => {
    for (const table of ['agency_revenue_entries', 'agency_revenue_rule_versions', 'agency_revenue_scopes', 'payment_allocations', 'payments', 'receivables']) {
      await admin.query(`DELETE FROM ${table}`);
    }
  };

  async function makeSale(opts: { destination?: string; origin?: string; total?: number; status?: string; saleDate?: string; items?: Array<{ supplier?: boolean; price: number }> }) {
    const total = opts.total ?? 1000;
    const sale = (
      await admin.query<{ id: string }>(
        `INSERT INTO sales (agency_id, customer_id, user_id, amount, discount, total, status, destination_text, origin_text, sale_date)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 0, $4, $5, $6, $7, COALESCE($8::date, CURRENT_DATE)) RETURNING id`,
        [agencyA, customerA, ids.agentA, total, opts.status ?? 'CONFIRMED', opts.destination ?? null, opts.origin ?? null, opts.saleDate ?? null],
      )
    ).rows[0]!.id;
    for (const [index, item] of (opts.items ?? []).entries()) {
      await admin.query(
        `INSERT INTO sale_items (agency_id, sale_id, description, supplier_id, qty, unit_price, line_total, line_margin)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 1, $5, $5, 0)`,
        [agencyA, sale, `Item ${index}`, item.supplier ? supplier : null, item.price],
      );
    }
    return sale;
  }
  async function makeReceivable(saleId: string, dueOffset: number, amount: number, paid = 0, installment = 1) {
    const id = (
      await admin.query<{ id: string }>(
        `INSERT INTO receivables (agency_id, sale_id, customer_id, description, amount, due_at, due_date, status, installment_number, installment_count)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'Parcela', $4, ($5::date)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'UTC', $5::date, $6, $7, 2) RETURNING id`,
        [agencyA, saleId, customerA, amount, civil(dueOffset), paid > 0 ? 'PARTIALLY_PAID' : 'OPEN', installment],
      )
    ).rows[0]!.id;
    if (paid > 0) {
      const payment = (await admin.query<{ id: string }>(`INSERT INTO payments (agency_id, direction, amount, occurred_at, created_by) VALUES ($1, 'IN', $2, now(), $3) RETURNING id`, [agencyA, paid, ids.adminA])).rows[0]!.id;
      await admin.query(`INSERT INTO payment_allocations (agency_id, payment_id, receivable_id, amount) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)`, [agencyA, payment, id, paid]);
    }
    return id;
  }

  describe('access', () => {
    it('requires authentication and MANAGER+, validates filters', async () => {
      for (const path of ['receivables-projection', 'revenue-forecast', 'supplier-balances', 'commissions-projection']) {
        expect((await get(null, `/reports/${path}`)).status).toBe(401);
        expect((await get(ids.agentA, `/reports/${path}`)).status).toBe(403);
        expect((await get(ids.managerA, `/reports/${path}`)).status).toBe(200);
      }
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection?saleFrom=2026-13-99x')).status).toBe(400);
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection?agencyId=' + agencyB)).status).toBe(400);
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection?status=HACK')).status).toBe(400);
    });
  });

  describe('source restrictions apply to the projections too', () => {
    it('a role restriction or a per-user REVOKE blocks the receivables projection and the commissions projection', async () => {
      await reset();
      await admin.query('DELETE FROM permission_restrictions');
      await admin.query('DELETE FROM user_permission_overrides');
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection')).status).toBe(200);
      await admin.query(`INSERT INTO user_permission_overrides (agency_id, user_id, resource, action, effect, reason, created_by_user_id)
        VALUES ($1, $2, 'financial', 'receivables.read', 'REVOKE', 'teste', $3)`, [agencyA, ids.managerA, ids.adminA]);
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection')).status).toBe(403);
      expect((await get(ids.adminA, '/reports/receivables-projection')).status).toBe(200);
      await admin.query('DELETE FROM user_permission_overrides');
      await admin.query(`INSERT INTO permission_restrictions (agency_id, role, resource, action, created_by_user_id) VALUES ($1, 'MANAGER', 'employees', 'view', $2)`, [agencyA, ids.adminA]);
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/commissions-projection')).status).toBe(403);
      await admin.query('DELETE FROM permission_restrictions');
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/commissions-projection')).status).toBe(200);
    });
    it('filters a report cannot honour are refused instead of silently ignored', async () => {
      for (const path of ['receivables-projection', 'commissions-projection']) {
        expect((await get(ids.managerA, `/reports/${path}?supplierId=${supplier}`)).status).toBe(400);
        expect((await get(ids.managerA, `/reports/${path}?productId=x`)).status).toBe(400);
      }
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, `/reports/revenue-forecast?supplierId=${supplier}`)).status).toBe(200);
    });
  });

  describe('receivables projection', () => {
    it('open balance after a partial payment, late separated, filtered by destination', async () => {
      await reset();
      const gramado = await makeSale({ destination: 'Gramado, RS', total: 1000 });
      await makeReceivable(gramado, -5, 500, 200);
      await makeReceivable(gramado, 20, 500, 0, 2);
      const other = await makeSale({ destination: 'Lisboa', total: 800 });
      await makeReceivable(other, 20, 800);

      const all = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection');
      expect(all.body.late).toEqual({ count: 1, openBalance: 300 });
      expect(all.body.totalOpen).toBe(1600);

      const filtered = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection?destination=gramado');
      expect(filtered.body.late.openBalance).toBe(300);
      expect(filtered.body.totalOpen).toBe(800);
      expect(filtered.body.byMonth.reduce((sum: number, row: any) => sum + row.openBalance, 0)).toBe(500);
    });
    it('a LIKE wildcard in a filter is literal, and CSV is formula-safe with private headers', async () => {
      await reset();
      const sale = await makeSale({ destination: 'Gramado' });
      await makeReceivable(sale, 10, 100);
      expect((await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection?destination=%25')).body.totalOpen).toBe(0);
      const csv = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/receivables-projection?format=csv');
      expect(csv.status).toBe(200);
      expect(csv.headers['content-type']).toBe('text/csv; charset=utf-8');
      expect(csv.headers['cache-control']).toBe('private, no-store');
      expect(csv.text.replace(/^﻿/, '').split('\r\n')[0]).toBe('period,openBalance,count');
    });
  });

  describe('revenue forecast and supplier balances', () => {
    it('separates contracted / projected / undefined, ignores PENDING_RULE, and exposes the supplier difference', async () => {
      await reset();
      const rule = await post(ids.adminA, '/agency-revenue/rules', { supplierId: supplier, mode: 'PERCENTAGE', baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', value: 10, entitlementEvent: 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION', reason: 'x' });
      expect(rule.status).toBe(201);
      // contracted: confirmed sale, ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION -> ENTITLED (10% of 1000 = 100)
      const confirmed = await makeSale({ items: [{ supplier: true, price: 1000 }] });
      await post(ids.managerA, `/sales/${confirmed}/agency-revenue/calculate`);
      // projected: pending sale -> FORECAST for ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION has no date => undefined
      const pending = await makeSale({ status: 'PENDING', items: [{ supplier: true, price: 400 }] });
      await post(ids.managerA, `/sales/${pending}/agency-revenue/calculate`);
      // no rule for the item without supplier -> PENDING_RULE, never counted
      const noRule = await makeSale({ items: [{ price: 999 }] });
      await post(ids.managerA, `/sales/${noRule}/agency-revenue/calculate`);

      const forecast = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/revenue-forecast');
      expect(forecast.body.contracted.total).toBe(100);
      expect(forecast.body.undefined.total).toBe(40);
      expect(forecast.body.projected.total).toBe(0);
      expect(forecast.body.grandTotal).toBe(140);

      const entry = (await admin.query<{ id: string }>(`SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE sale_id = $1`, [confirmed])).rows[0]!.id;
      await post(ids.adminA, `/agency-revenue/entries/${entry}/receive`, { amount: 90 });
      const balances = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/supplier-balances');
      expect(balances.body.rows).toEqual([{ supplierId: supplier, expected: 100, received: 90, difference: 10 }]);
      const after = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/revenue-forecast');
      expect(after.body.contracted.total).toBe(0);
    });
    it('a settlement-based FORECAST is projected on the last receivable due date', async () => {
      await reset();
      await post(ids.adminA, '/agency-revenue/rules', { supplierId: supplier, mode: 'PERCENTAGE', baseSelector: 'LINE_TOTAL', value: 10, entitlementEvent: 'ON_FULL_SETTLEMENT', reason: 'x' });
      const sale = await makeSale({ items: [{ supplier: true, price: 1000 }] });
      await makeReceivable(sale, 10, 500);
      await makeReceivable(sale, 40, 500, 0, 2);
      await post(ids.managerA, `/sales/${sale}/agency-revenue/calculate`);
      const forecast = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/revenue-forecast');
      expect(forecast.body.projected.total).toBe(100);
      expect(forecast.body.projected.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: civil(40).slice(0, 7), amount: 100 }]);
    });
    it("agency B sees none of agency A's rows", async () => {
      expect((await get(ids.adminB, '/reports/revenue-forecast')).body.grandTotal).toBe(0);
      expect((await get(ids.adminB, '/reports/supplier-balances')).body.rows).toEqual([]);
      expect((await get(ids.adminB, '/reports/receivables-projection')).body.totalOpen).toBe(0);
    });
    it('revenue CSV protects formulas in the supplier column', async () => {
      const csv = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/supplier-balances?format=csv');
      expect(csv.status).toBe(200);
      expect(csv.text).toContain('supplierId,expected,received,difference');
    });
  });

  describe('commission projection', () => {
    it('is independent from the agency revenue and returns zeros when there are no entries', async () => {
      await reset();
      const res = await get(ids.managerA, '/reports/commissions-projection');
      expect(res.body).toEqual({ forecast: 0, released: 0, paid: 0, releasedNotPaid: 0, notYetReleased: 0 });
    });
  });
});
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  projectCommissions,
  projectReceivables,
  projectRevenue,
  supplierBalances,
  type CommissionRow,
  type ReceivableRow,
  type RevenueRow,
} from '../src/report-projections';

const receivable = (over: Partial<ReceivableRow>): ReceivableRow => ({
  id: 'r',
  saleId: 's',
  dueDate: '2026-11-10',
  amount: 100,
  paid: 0,
  status: 'OPEN',
  ...over,
});

describe('projectReceivables (customer receivables by due date)', () => {
  it('open balance is amount minus allocations, bucketed by due date, late separated', () => {
    const result = projectReceivables(
      [
        receivable({ id: 'a', dueDate: '2026-10-20', amount: 500, paid: 200, status: 'PARTIALLY_PAID' }),
        receivable({ id: 'b', dueDate: '2026-11-10', amount: 300 }),
        receivable({ id: 'c', dueDate: '2026-11-25', amount: 200, paid: 50, status: 'PARTIALLY_PAID' }),
        receivable({ id: 'd', dueDate: '2026-12-05', amount: 100 }),
      ],
      '2026-10-24',
    );
    expect(result.late).toEqual({ count: 1, openBalance: 300 });
    expect(result.byMonth).toEqual([
      { month: '2026-11', openBalance: 450, count: 2 },
      { month: '2026-12', openBalance: 100, count: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(result.totalOpen).toBe(850);
  });

  it('a due date equal to today is not late; settled and cancelled rows are ignored', () => {
    const result = projectReceivables(
      [
        receivable({ id: 'a', dueDate: '2026-10-24', amount: 100 }),
        receivable({ id: 'b', dueDate: '2026-10-01', amount: 100, paid: 100, status: 'PAID' }),
        receivable({ id: 'c', dueDate: '2026-10-01', amount: 100, status: 'CANCELLED' }),
      ],
      '2026-10-24',
    );
    expect(result.late).toEqual({ count: 0, openBalance: 0 });
    expect(result.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: '2026-10', openBalance: 100, count: 1 }]);
    expect(result.totalOpen).toBe(100);
  });

  it('works in cents (no floating point drift)', () => {
    const result = projectReceivables(
      [receivable({ amount: 0.3, paid: 0.1 }), receivable({ id: 'x', amount: 0.2 })],
      '2026-10-24',
    );
    expect(result.totalOpen).toBe(0.4);
  });
});

const revenue = (over: Partial<RevenueRow>): RevenueRow => ({
  entryId: 'e',
  supplierId: 's1',
  status: 'FORECAST',
  amount: 100,
  receivedAmount: null,
  entitledOn: null,
  expectedOn: null,
  ...over,
});

describe('projectRevenue (contracted / projected / undefined; never PENDING_RULE)', () => {
  it('classifies ENTITLED/AVAILABLE as CONTRATADO, FORECAST with a date as PROJETADO, FORECAST without as INDEFINIDO', () => {
    const result = projectRevenue([
      revenue({ entryId: '1', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 70, entitledOn: '2026-10-20' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '2', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 45, entitledOn: '2026-10-22' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '3', status: 'AVAILABLE', amount: 120, entitledOn: '2026-10-25' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '4', status: 'FORECAST', amount: 80, expectedOn: '2026-12-02' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '5', status: 'FORECAST', amount: 60 }),
    ]);
    expect(result.contracted.total).toBe(235);
    expect(result.projected.total).toBe(80);
    expect(result.undefined.total).toBe(60);
    expect(result.contracted.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: '2026-10', amount: 235 }]);
    expect(result.projected.byMonth).toEqual([{ month: '2026-12', amount: 80 }]);
    expect(result.grandTotal).toBe(375);
  });

  it('never counts PENDING_RULE, CANCELLED or RECEIVED entries', () => {
    const result = projectRevenue([
      revenue({ entryId: '1', status: 'PENDING_RULE', amount: 0 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '2', status: 'CANCELLED', amount: 50 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '3', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 90, receivedAmount: 90 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '4', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 10, entitledOn: '2026-10-20' }),
    ]);
    expect(result.grandTotal).toBe(10);
  });

  it('groups by supplier too', () => {
    const result = projectRevenue([
      revenue({ entryId: '1', supplierId: 'A', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 100, entitledOn: '2026-10-20' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '2', supplierId: 'B', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 50, entitledOn: '2026-10-21' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '3', supplierId: 'A', status: 'FORECAST', amount: 25 }),
    ]);
    expect(result.bySupplier).toEqual([
      { supplierId: 'A', contracted: 100, projected: 0, undefined: 25 },
      { supplierId: 'B', contracted: 50, projected: 0, undefined: 0 },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('supplierBalances (expected x received x difference, per supplier)', () => {
  it('shows the open difference and keeps unreceived entitled money visible', () => {
    const result = supplierBalances([
      revenue({ entryId: '1', supplierId: 'A', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 100, receivedAmount: 90 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '2', supplierId: 'A', status: 'ENTITLED', amount: 40, entitledOn: '2026-10-20' }),
      revenue({ entryId: '3', supplierId: 'B', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 30, receivedAmount: 30 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '4', supplierId: 'A', status: 'PENDING_RULE', amount: 0 }),
      revenue({ entryId: '5', supplierId: 'A', status: 'CANCELLED', amount: 500 }),
    ]);
    expect(result).toEqual([
      { supplierId: 'A', expected: 140, received: 90, difference: 50 },
      { supplierId: 'B', expected: 30, received: 30, difference: 0 },
    ]);
  });
});

const commission = (over: Partial<CommissionRow>): CommissionRow => ({
  entryId: 'c',
  amount: 100,
  released: 0,
  paid: 0,
  status: 'APPROVED',
  ...over,
});

describe('projectCommissions (forecast x released x paid; independent of the agency revenue)', () => {
  it('separates what is forecast, what is released by customer receipts and what was paid', () => {
    const result = projectCommissions([
      commission({ entryId: '1', amount: 1000, released: 300, paid: 100 }),
      commission({ entryId: '2', amount: 200, released: 200, paid: 200, status: 'PAID' }),
      commission({ entryId: '3', amount: 500, released: 0 }),
      commission({ entryId: '4', amount: 999, released: 999, status: 'CANCELLED' }),
    ]);
    expect(result).toEqual({
      forecast: 1700,
      released: 500,
      paid: 300,
      releasedNotPaid: 200,
      notYetReleased: 1200,
    });
  });

  it('never lets released exceed the amount or paid exceed released', () => {
    const result = projectCommissions([commission({ amount: 100, released: 150, paid: 130 })]);
    expect(result.released).toBe(100);
    expect(result.paid).toBe(100);
    expect(result.releasedNotPaid).toBe(0);
  });
});

/**
 * Sale form field registry (ADR-008).
 *
 * The registry lives in code: validators and types are reviewed and tested here,
 * while each agency's choice of which fields a category shows (and requires) is
 * stored in `sale_category_fields` and references a field by `key` only. The
 * Lite product keeps its own copy of this list (services/api-lite/src/sale-form-fields.ts);
 * a parity test guarantees every `availableIn` LITE field also exists here.
 */

import { FormFieldError, ValidationError } from './errors';

export type SaleFormFieldType = 'text' | 'date' | 'integer' | 'money' | 'enum' | 'passenger_list' | 'price_components';
export type SaleFormFieldScope = 'customer' | 'sale' | 'sale_passenger';
export type SaleFormFieldClassification = 'LEGAL' | 'MARKET' | 'SUGGESTION';
export type SaleFormProduct = 'FULL' | 'LITE';

export interface SaleFormFieldDefinition {
  key: string;
  scope: SaleFormFieldScope;
  label: string;
  type: SaleFormFieldType;
  group: string;
  /** `column`: first-class sales column (reports). `modality`: key in sales.modality_data. */
  storage: 'column' | 'modality';
  /** Locked fields are not configurable: always enabled, never required by the profile. */
  locked: boolean;
  /** Documentation metadata only (ADR-008): it never changes behavior. */
  classification: SaleFormFieldClassification;
  availableIn: readonly SaleFormProduct[];
  maxLength?: number;
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
}

const BOTH: readonly SaleFormProduct[] = ['FULL', 'LITE'];

export const SALE_FORM_FIELDS: readonly SaleFormFieldDefinition[] = [
  { key: 'sale_date', scope: 'sale', label: 'Data da venda', type: 'date', group: 'Venda', storage: 'column', locked: true, classification: 'MARKET', availableIn: BOTH },

  { key: 'passenger_count', scope: 'sale', label: 'Quantidade de passageiros', type: 'integer', group: 'Viagem', storage: 'column', locked: false, classification: 'MARKET', availableIn: BOTH, min: 1, max: 999 },
  { key: 'locator', scope: 'sale', label: 'Localizador / PNR', type: 'text', group: 'Reserva', storage: 'modality', locked: false, classification: 'MARKET', availableIn: BOTH, maxLength: 60 },
