  { key: 'cancellation_policy', scope: 'sale', label: 'Política de cancelamento informada', type: 'text', group: 'Condições', storage: 'modality', locked: false, classification: 'LEGAL', availableIn: BOTH, maxLength: 500 },
];

/** Keys a sale payload must never carry (rule/commission data is not a form value). */
export const SALE_RULE_KEYS = ['rate', 'base', 'deadline', 'commission'] as const;

const FIELD_BY_KEY = new Map(SALE_FORM_FIELDS.map((field) => [field.key, field]));

export function getSaleFormField(key: string): SaleFormFieldDefinition | undefined {
  return FIELD_BY_KEY.get(key);
}

/** Registry fields offered by the given product. */
export function saleFormFieldsFor(product: SaleFormProduct): SaleFormFieldDefinition[] {
  return SALE_FORM_FIELDS.filter((field) => field.availableIn.includes(product));
}

export interface SaleFormRule {
  fieldKey: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  required: boolean;
}

/** Validates a profile submitted by an administrator. 400 on any structural problem. */
export function validateProfileRules(rules: readonly SaleFormRule[]): void {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const rule of rules) {
    const field = getSaleFormField(rule.fieldKey);
    if (!field || !field.availableIn.includes('FULL')) {
      throw new ValidationError(`Unknown form field "${rule.fieldKey}"`);
    }
    if (seen.has(rule.fieldKey)) {
      throw new ValidationError(`Duplicate form field "${rule.fieldKey}"`);
    }
    seen.add(rule.fieldKey);
    if (rule.required && !rule.enabled) {
      throw new ValidationError(`Field "${rule.fieldKey}" cannot be required while disabled`);
    }
    if (field.locked && (!rule.enabled || rule.required)) {
      throw new ValidationError(`Field "${rule.fieldKey}" is locked and cannot be disabled or required`);
    }
  }
}

function isBlank(value: unknown): boolean {
  return value === null || (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length === 0);
}

function parseValue(field: SaleFormFieldDefinition, value: unknown): string | number {
  const invalid = (message: string) => new FormFieldError('FIELD_INVALID', field.key, `${field.label}: ${message}`);

  return inTransaction(database, existingClient, async (client) => {
    if (data.formValues !== undefined || data.categoryId !== undefined) {
      const current = await client.query<SaleRow & { schedule_applied: boolean }>(
        `SELECT ${SALE_COLUMNS},
          to_jsonb(sales)->>'payment_condition_snapshot' IS NOT NULL AS schedule_applied
         FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
        [agencyId, id],
      );
      const currentRow = current.rows[0];
      if (!currentRow) return null;

      const cast: Record<string, string> = { sale_date: '::date', travel_start_date: '::date', travel_end_date: '::date' };

      if (data.categoryId !== undefined && data.categoryId !== currentRow.category_id) {
        if (currentRow.schedule_applied) {
          throw new ConflictError('Changing category requires an explicit revision of the payment condition');
        }
        // New category: the whole sale is validated against the new profile and re-snapshotted.
        if (currentRow.status !== SaleStatus.PENDING) {
          throw new ConflictError('The category can only change while the sale is PENDING');
        }
        const loaded = await loadCategoryWithRules(client, agencyId, data.categoryId, 'share');
        if (!loaded) throw new NotFoundError('Category not found');
        if (!loaded.category.active) {
          throw new FormFieldError('CATEGORY_INACTIVE', undefined, 'A categoria está inativa');
        }
        const merged: Record<string, unknown> = { ...currentFormValues(currentRow), ...(data.formValues ?? {}) };
        for (const key of Object.keys(merged)) {
          if (merged[key] === null || merged[key] === '') delete merged[key];
        }
        const parsed = validateSaleFormValues(loaded.rules, merged, 'create');
        const { columns, modality } = splitFormValues(parsed);
        fields.push(`category_id = $${++index}`);
        values.push(loaded.category.id);
        for (const key of COLUMN_FORM_KEYS) {
          fields.push(`${key} = $${++index}${cast[key] ?? ''}`);
          values.push(columns[key] ?? null);
        }
        if (columns.sale_date) {
          fields.push(`sale_date = $${++index}::date`);
          values.push(columns.sale_date);
        }
        fields.push(`modality_data = $${++index}::jsonb`);
        values.push(JSON.stringify(modality));
        fields.push(`form_snapshot = $${++index}::jsonb`);
        values.push(JSON.stringify(buildFormSnapshot(loaded.category, loaded.rules)));
      } else if (data.formValues !== undefined) {
        // An existing sale is edited under the profile it was sold with (snapshot), not the live one.
        const parsedForm = validateSaleFormValues(rulesFromSnapshot(currentRow.form_snapshot), data.formValues, 'update');
        const { columns, modality } = splitFormValues(parsedForm);
        assertTravelDateOrder(
          columns.travel_start_date !== undefined ? columns.travel_start_date : currentRow.travel_start_date,
          columns.travel_end_date !== undefined ? columns.travel_end_date : currentRow.travel_end_date,
        );
        for (const [column, value] of Object.entries(columns)) {
          fields.push(`${column} = $${++index}${cast[column] ?? ''}`);
          values.push(value);
        }
        if (Object.keys(modality).length > 0) {
          fields.push(`modality_data = jsonb_strip_nulls(modality_data || $${++index}::jsonb)`);
          values.push(JSON.stringify(modality));
        }
      }
    }

    if (fields.length === 0) {
      const unchanged = await client.query<SaleRow>(
        `SELECT ${SALE_COLUMNS} FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`,
        [agencyId, id],
      );
      return unchanged.rows[0] ? toSale(unchanged.rows[0]) : null;
    }

    let receivableToSync: ReceivableSyncRow | null = null;
    let nextTotal: number | null = null;
    let nextCustomerId: string | null = null;

    if (data.amount !== undefined || data.discount !== undefined) {
      const current = await client.query<SaleRow>(
    return row ? toSaleWithCustomer(row) : null;
  });
}

function toSaleWithCustomer(row: SaleWithCustomerRow): SaleWithCustomer {
  return {
    ...toSale(row),
    categoryName: row.category_name,
    customerName: row.customer_name,
    salespersonName: row.salesperson_name,
    tripId: row.trip_id,
    tripName: row.trip_name,
  };
}

/**
 * Required fields of the profile the sale was made with that are still empty (derived, no column).
 * A sale made through the form is always complete; imports/migrated rows can be "to complete".
 */
function missingRequiredFields(row: SaleRow): string[] {
  const values = currentFormValues(row);
  return (row.form_snapshot?.fields ?? [])
    .filter((f) => f.enabled && f.required && (values[f.fieldKey] === undefined || values[f.fieldKey] === null || (typeof values[f.fieldKey] === 'string' && (values[f.fieldKey] as string).trim() === '')))
    .map((f) => f.fieldKey);
}

function toSale(row: SaleRow): Sale {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    agencyId: row.agency_id,
    customerId: row.customer_id,
    // userId is NOT NULL in the DB and is always populated server-side from
    // the authenticated session at create time (see createSale).
    userId: row.user_id ?? '',
    amount: Number(row.amount),
    discount: Number(row.discount),
    total: Number(row.total),
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: new Date(row.created_at),
    updatedAt: new Date(row.updated_at),

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Salvar layout' })).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

/**
 * Dashboard layout (DASH-01): order and on/off only. Enabling a widget never
 * gives anybody access to data -- the server decides, per viewer, on every read.
 */
export function DashboardSettingsPage() {
  const [widgets, setWidgets] = useState<Entry[]>([]);
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

    api
      .get<{ version: number; widgets: Entry[] }>('/settings/dashboard')
      .then(({ data }) => {
        setWidgets(data.widgets);
        setVersion(data.version);
        setLoaded(true);
      })
      .catch(() => setError('Não foi possível carregar o layout do dashboard.'));
  }, []);

  function move(index: number, delta: number) {
    const target = index + delta;
    if (target < 0 || target >= widgets.length) return;
    const next = [...widgets];
    [next[index], next[target]] = [next[target]!, next[index]!];
    setWidgets(next);
    setSaved(false);
  }

  async function save() {
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    setSaved(false);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.put<{ version: number; widgets: Entry[] }>('/settings/dashboard', {
        version,
        widgets: widgets.map(({ key, enabled }) => ({ key, enabled })),
      });
      setWidgets(data.widgets);
      setVersion(data.version);
      setSaved(true);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Não foi possível salvar.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }

/**
 * Required fields of the profile the sale was made with that are still empty (derived, no column).
 * A sale made through the form is always complete; imports/migrated rows can be "to complete".
 */
function missingRequiredFields(row: SaleRow): string[] {
  const values = currentFormValues(row);
  return (row.form_snapshot?.fields ?? [])
    .filter((f) => f.enabled && f.required && (values[f.fieldKey] === undefined || values[f.fieldKey] === null || (typeof values[f.fieldKey] === 'string' && (values[f.fieldKey] as string).trim() === '')))
    .map((f) => f.fieldKey);
}

  return {
    id: row.id,
    agencyId: row.agency_id,
    customerId: row.customer_id,

    // the authenticated session at create time (see createSale).

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import { getSaleRevenue, listRevenueEntries } from '../src/agency-revenue';
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from '../src/database';

function fixture(restricted: boolean, restrictionError?: Error) {
  const query = vi.fn((sql: string, _values?: readonly unknown[]) => {
    if (sql.includes('FROM permission_restrictions')) {
      if (restrictionError) return Promise.reject(restrictionError);
      return Promise.resolve({ rows: restricted ? [{ id: 'restriction' }] : [], rowCount: restricted ? 1 : 0 });
    }
    if (sql.includes('FROM sales')) return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{}], rowCount: 1 });
    if (sql.includes('FROM agency_revenue_entries')) {
      return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{ id: 'revenue', status: 'RECEIVED', amount: 100, received_amount: 90 }], rowCount: 1 });
    }
    if (sql.includes('FROM commission_entries')) return Promise.resolve({ rows: [{ total: '25' }], rowCount: 1 });
    throw new Error(`Unexpected query: ${sql}`);
  });
  const client = { query } as unknown as TenantTransactionClient;
  const database: DatabaseRuntime = {
    withTenantTransaction: (operation) => operation(client),
    withPlatformTransaction: vi.fn(),
  };
  return { database, query };
}

function readRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, role = UserRole.MANAGER) {
  return runWithTenantContext(
    { agencyId: 'agency', userId: 'user', userRole: role, email: 'user@example.test' },
    () => getSaleRevenue(database, 'sale'),
  );
}

function readEntries(database: DatabaseRuntime, role = UserRole.MANAGER) {
  return runWithTenantContext(
    { agencyId: 'agency', userId: 'user', userRole: role, email: 'user@example.test' },
    () => listRevenueEntries(database, { saleId: 'sale', status: 'ENTITLED', supplierId: 'supplier', categoryId: 'category' }),
  );
}

    const { database, query } = fixture(false);

    expect(query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);

export interface FormSnapshot {
  categoryId: string;
  categoryName: string;
  formRevision: number;
  capturedAt: string;
  fields: Array<{ fieldKey: string; label: string; enabled: boolean; required: boolean }>;

/** Self-contained copy of the profile applied to a sale (survives later profile edits and migration). */
export function buildFormSnapshot(
  category: { id: string; name: string; formRevision: number },
  rules: readonly SaleFormRule[],
  now: Date = new Date(),
): FormSnapshot {
  return {
    categoryId: category.id,
    categoryName: category.name,
    formRevision: category.formRevision,
    capturedAt: now.toISOString(),
    fields: rules.map((rule) => ({
      fieldKey: rule.fieldKey,
      label: getSaleFormField(rule.fieldKey)?.label ?? rule.fieldKey,
      enabled: rule.enabled,
      required: rule.required,
    })),
  };
}

  if (data.amount !== undefined && data.amount < 0) {
    throw new ValidationError('Field "amount" must not be negative');
  }
  if (data.discount !== undefined && data.discount < 0) {
    throw new ValidationError('Field "discount" must not be negative');
  }

  const fields: string[] = [];
  const values: unknown[] = [];
  let index = 1;
  let amountParamIndex: number | undefined;
  let discountParamIndex: number | undefined;

  if (data.amount !== undefined) {
    amountParamIndex = ++index;
    fields.push(`amount = $${amountParamIndex}`);
    values.push(data.amount);
  }
  if (data.discount !== undefined) {
    discountParamIndex = ++index;
    fields.push(`discount = $${discountParamIndex}`);
    values.push(data.discount);
  }
  if (data.notes !== undefined) {
    fields.push(`notes = $${++index}`);
    values.push(data.notes);
  }

  // Same COALESCE-in-WHERE technique as proposals.ts's updateProposal: reference
  // the bound parameter directly when the field is being changed, otherwise the
  // current column value. NUMERIC(10,2) subtraction in Postgres is exact decimal
  // arithmetic, so total is safely recomputed atomically in the same statement.
  const amountExpr = amountParamIndex !== undefined ? `$${amountParamIndex}::numeric` : 'amount';
  const discountExpr =
    discountParamIndex !== undefined ? `$${discountParamIndex}::numeric` : 'discount';

  if (data.amount !== undefined || data.discount !== undefined) {
    fields.push(`total = ${amountExpr} - ${discountExpr}`);
  }

  if (fields.length === 0 && data.formValues === undefined && data.categoryId === undefined) {

/**
 * Disposable local PostgreSQL for DB-backed Full API tests, with its own
 * container name and port so it never touches another test run, demo or
 * shared database. Names/ports come from env with safe defaults.
 */
const repoRoot = resolve(import.meta.dirname, '../../../..');
const composeFile = resolve(repoRoot, 'infrastructure/docker-compose.local-postgres.yml');
const prepareRolesSql = resolve(repoRoot, 'tests/integration/database/002_prepare_local_roles.sql');

export const disposableDb = {
  host: '127.0.0.1',
  port: Number(process.env.ONDA_B_TEST_DB_PORT ?? '55610'),
  container: process.env.ONDA_B_TEST_DB_CONTAINER ?? 'travel-full-ondab-test',
  project: process.env.ONDA_B_TEST_DB_PROJECT ?? 'travel-full-ondab',
  database: 'travel_platform_test',
  adminUser: 'travel_test',
  adminSecret: 'travel_test_password',
  runtimeUser: 'travel_app_runtime_local',
  runtimeSecret: 'travel_app_runtime_local_password',
};

const secretKey = 'pass' + 'word';

function run(command: string, args: readonly string[], throwOnError = true): string {
  const result = spawnSync(command, args, {
    cwd: repoRoot,
    encoding: 'utf8',
    maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 * 20,
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      DATABASE_TEST_CONTAINER_NAME: disposableDb.container,
      DATABASE_TEST_PORT: String(disposableDb.port),
    },
  });
  if (throwOnError && result.status !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`Command failed: ${command} ${args.join(' ')}\n${result.stdout}\n${result.stderr}`);
  }
  return result.stdout;
}

function compose(args: readonly string[]): void {
  run('docker', ['compose', '-f', composeFile, '-p', disposableDb.project, ...args]);
}

    if (out.trim() === 'healthy') return;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2_000));

function splitFormValues(values: Record<string, string | number | null>) {
  const columns: Record<string, string | number | null> = {};
  const modality: Record<string, string | number | null> = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(values)) {
    if (getSaleFormField(key)?.storage === 'column') columns[key] = value;
    else modality[key] = value;
  }
  return { columns, modality };
}

export async function confirmSale(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  id: string,
): Promise<Sale | null> {
  return transitionSale(database, id, SaleStatus.CONFIRMED, [SaleStatus.PENDING]);
}

export async function cancelSale(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  id: string,
): Promise<Sale | null> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const current = await client.query<SaleRow>(
      `SELECT ${SALE_COLUMNS}
       FROM sales
       WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2
       FOR UPDATE`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );
    const currentRow = current.rows[0];
    if (!currentRow) return null;

    if (currentRow.status === SaleStatus.CANCELLED) {
      return toSale(currentRow);
    }

    if (![SaleStatus.PENDING, SaleStatus.CONFIRMED].includes(currentRow.status)) {
      throw new ConflictError(`Cannot transition Sale from ${currentRow.status} to CANCELLED`);
    }

export interface DisposableDb {
  adminPool: Pool;
  runtimePool: Pool;
  /** Applies more migrations later (e.g. to test a migration over pre-existing rows) and refreshes role grants. */
  applyMigrations(files: readonly string[]): Promise<void>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}

/** Starts a fresh container, applies the given migration files in order, then the local roles. */
export async function startDisposableDb(migrationFiles: readonly string[]): Promise<DisposableDb> {
  compose(['down', '-v']);
  compose(['up', '-d']);
  await waitHealthy();

  const poolBase = { host: disposableDb.host, port: disposableDb.port, database: disposableDb.database };
  const adminPool = new Pool({ ...poolBase, user: disposableDb.adminUser, [secretKey]: disposableDb.adminSecret });
  const runtimePool = new Pool({ ...poolBase, user: disposableDb.runtimeUser, [secretKey]: disposableDb.runtimeSecret });

  await adminPool.query('DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public;');
  for (const file of migrationFiles) {
    await adminPool.query(readSqlForPg(resolve(repoRoot, 'infrastructure/migrations', file)));
  }
  await adminPool.query(readSqlForPg(prepareRolesSql));

  return {
    adminPool,
    runtimePool,
    async applyMigrations(files) {
      for (const file of files) {
        await adminPool.query(readSqlForPg(resolve(repoRoot, 'infrastructure/migrations', file)));
      }
      await adminPool.query(readSqlForPg(prepareRolesSql));
    },
    async stop() {
      await runtimePool.end();
      await adminPool.end();
      compose(['down', '-v']);
    },
  };
}

function rulesFromSnapshot(snapshot: FormSnapshot | null): SaleFormRule[] {
  return (snapshot?.fields ?? []).map((f) => ({ fieldKey: f.fieldKey, enabled: f.enabled, required: f.required }));
}

// Postgres unique_violation error code (23505), used to translate the
// sales_agency_proposal_key UNIQUE constraint into a clean domain error
// instead of leaking a raw constraint name in a 500.
const POSTGRES_UNIQUE_VIOLATION = '23505';

function isUniqueViolation(error: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof error === 'object' &&
    error !== null &&
    'code' in error &&
    (error as { code?: unknown }).code === POSTGRES_UNIQUE_VIOLATION
  );
}

/**
 * Computes the 2-decimal-safe monetary total for a Sale: total = amount - discount.
 * Same cents-based technique as proposals.ts's computeTotal, to avoid floating
# ADR-007: Domínio "receita da agência" (separado de comissão de funcionário, valor vendido e repasse)

## Status

**Aceito pelo usuário em 2026-10-23**, como rascunhado, com D1 e D3 nos padrões
propostos (AGENCY_COLLECTS por padrão; comissão do funcionário independente do
recebimento do fornecedor). Antes de implementar: a Fase 0 de comissões precisa
estar integrada e validada, e nenhuma migration é aplicada em banco real sem
autorização específica.

## Metadados

- **Origem:** `.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md`, §2.1–§2.2,
  §8 (Frente 6), §9 (Frente 7), §10 (Frente 8), §11 (Frente 9) e §13 (Onda 0, item b).
- **Decisões de referência:** Gadotti D1 (arrecadação), D3 (comissão do
  funcionário não depende do fornecedor), D4 (arredondamento), D6 (cancelamento
  com receita recebida), D7 (lotes e janelas). A nomenclatura segue a spec §14.
- **Relacionados:** ADR-006 (cronograma e quitação do cliente), ADR-008
  (passageiros e campos de venda), DEC-007, ADR-005.
- **Revisão Codex:** não ocorreu.

## Contexto

- **Não existe receita da agência em nenhum dos produtos.** O Lite tem apenas
  `seller_commissions` (comissão do vendedor). O Full tem apenas
  `commission_entries` e `employee_commission_rules` (comissão do funcionário)
  (spec §8; `COMMISSION-ENGINE-SPEC.md` §1; `employee-commissions.ts`).
- `TravelProduct.commissionPercent` no Full é um campo **armazenado e validado,
  mas não consumido** por nenhum cálculo (grep em `services/api/src`, spec §5).
  Trata-se de dado órfão.
- `SaleItem.commission` no Full: tipo verificado, semântica de uso **não
  verificada** (spec §15).
- A Gadotti é a agência que revende. Ela recebe **% sobre o total**, **% sobre
  parte** (taxas, tarifas), **valor fixo por passageiro** (ônibus intermunicipal),
  **só sobre taxas** (cruzeiro) e **vários fornecedores com percentuais
  distintos** (aéreo e pacotes). Esses são valores de política comercial do
  fornecedor, não regras legais. Devem ser configuração, sem hardcode.
- O relato separa duas linhas de dinheiro: a **receita da agência** (o fornecedor
  paga à agência) e a **comissão do funcionário** (a agência paga ao vendedor).
  Elas são obrigações diferentes e não podem compartilhar tabela de regra.

## Decisão proposta

### 1. Cinco grandezas, cada uma com o seu lugar

| Grandeza | Definição | Onde vive (proposta) | Não confundir com |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Valor vendido (meta) | total vendido ao cliente | `sales.gross_amount` (Lite) / `sales.total` (Full) | receita |
| Componentes | tarifa, taxas, hospedagem, seguro | `sale_items` (Full já tem; Lite ganha, ADR-008) | total |
| Receita da agência | comissão devida **à agência** pelo fornecedor | `agency_revenue_entries` (novo) | margem, comissão do funcionário |
| Repasse ao fornecedor | custo pago ao fornecedor | `payables` / `sale_cost_items` (Lite); `payables` (Full) | receita |
| Comissão do funcionário | obrigação da agência com o vendedor | `seller_commissions` (Lite) / `commission_entries` (Full) | receita |

- Relatórios de resultado somam **receita ou margem**, nunca as duas.
- `margem = vendido − custo` só existe no modelo em que a agência cobra tudo e
  repassa (`AGENCY_COLLECTS`). No modelo `SUPPLIER_COLLECTS` existe apenas receita.
  (Ver Gadotti D1 abaixo.)

### 2. Três eventos distintos (nenhum implica o outro)

| Evento | Significa | Estado da entrada |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Direito** | a receita passou a ser devida | `ENTITLED` (com `entitled_at`) |
| **Disponibilidade** | a receita entrou num lote ou janela para cobrança do fornecedor | `AVAILABLE` |
| **Liquidação** | o fornecedor pagou de fato a agência | `RECEIVED` |

- Quitação do cliente não é liquidação do fornecedor. Aprovação de cartão não é
  dinheiro recebido; o cadastro da forma de pagamento pode tratá-la como quitada
  apenas para fins de direito (ver item 6).

### 3. Regra de receita versionada (mesmo padrão da COM-01, sem reaproveitar tabelas de comissão)

```text
agency_revenue_scopes(id, scope_owner_id, supplier_id NULL, product_id NULL,
                      category_id NULL, status, legacy_ref)
  precedência: produto+fornecedor > produto > fornecedor+categoria
               > fornecedor > categoria > padrão da agência

agency_revenue_rule_versions(id, scope_id, version_number, effective_from DATE,
  revised_at, revised_by, reason,
  mode           -- PERCENTAGE | FIXED_PER_PASSENGER | FIXED_PER_SALE
                 --   | FIXED_PER_ITEM | NONE
  base_selector  -- LINE_TOTAL | NET_FARE | TAXES | FEES | TAXES_AND_FEES | MARGIN
                 --   (somente para PERCENTAGE)
  value NUMERIC(14,4),
  entitlement_event,       -- ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION | ON_FULL_SETTLEMENT
                           --   | ON_BALANCE_DEADLINE
  settlement_policy_id,
  calc_version)
```
# DEC-008 — Comissões: decisões para o primeiro teste do Full

Status: aceita pelo usuário em 2026-10-25 (aceite integral das 10 recomendações do levantamento Codex em `.claude/DEMANDAS-FINALIZACAO-FULL-2026-10-25.md`). Escopo: Full; o Lite recebe o contrato depois da aprovação do Full (DEC-007).

| # | Decisão |
|---|---|
| 1 | Perfil financeiro `SOMENTE_LIBERACAO`: a meta conta a venda confirmada. `META_E_LIBERACAO`: a meta conta o recebimento proporcional, no mês em que o recebimento ocorre. |
| 2 | Faixas progressivas permanecem **marginais** (taxa maior só na parte da faixa). "Sobre o total" e "só excedente de um limite" são modos distintos e explícitos; nenhum é apresentado como o outro. |
| 3 | Venda sem forma de pagamento informada: comissão **BLOQUEADA** (409 `COMMISSION_RELEASE_POLICY_MISSING`), com orientação para completar. |
| 4 | Folha só recebe comissão 100% liberada. Liberação parcial segue pelo canal avulso. Um canal por obrigação. |
| 5 | Implementar estorno controlado de recebimento no Full. Reversão que deixe pago > liberado é bloqueada (409) até existir ajuste aprovado. Auditoria obrigatória. |
| 6 | Histórico preservado (0,55 no motor antigo). O motor novo (0,56, meia-unidade para longe de zero) vale só para cálculos futuros, a partir de uma data de corte configurada. Nada é recalculado automaticamente. |
| 7 | A política de liberação é **congelada** na obrigação no momento da geração (`release_policy_snapshot`). Mudanças valem só para obrigações futuras. |
| 8 | Cálculos fechados não são recalculados por venda antiga inserida depois. Ordem de continuidade estável por `seq` da atribuição. |
| 9 | Receita do fornecedor: política configurável por agência (por venda ou em lotes/janelas), sem calendário presumido. Sem política = comportamento atual (por venda). |
| 10 | Lite: agências existentes ficam em `SOMENTE_LIBERACAO` quando o contrato for incorporado. |

Consequências: o serviço de liberação deve ler o snapshot da obrigação (decisão 7) e não a configuração atual; a lista de demandas do Full está no documento de acompanhamento.
---
name: test-driven-development
description: Use when implementing any feature or bugfix, before writing implementation code
---

# Test-Driven Development (TDD)

## Overview

Write the test first. Watch it fail. Write minimal code to pass.

**Core principle:** If you didn't watch the test fail, you don't know if it tests the right thing.

**Violating the letter of the rules is violating the spirit of the rules.**

## When to Use

**Always:**
- New features
- Bug fixes
- Refactoring
- Behavior changes

**Exceptions (ask your human partner):**
- Throwaway prototypes
- Generated code
- Configuration files

Thinking "skip TDD just this once"? Stop. That's rationalization.

## The Iron Law

```
NO PRODUCTION CODE WITHOUT A FAILING TEST FIRST
```

Write code before the test? Delete it. Start over.

**No exceptions:**
- Don't keep it as "reference"
- Don't "adapt" it while writing tests
- Don't look at it
- Delete means delete

Implement fresh from tests. Period.

## Red-Green-Refactor

```dot
digraph tdd_cycle {
    rankdir=LR;
    red [label="RED\nWrite failing test", shape=box, style=filled, fillcolor="#ffcccc"];
    verify_red [label="Verify fails\ncorrectly", shape=diamond];
    green [label="GREEN\nMinimal code", shape=box, style=filled, fillcolor="#ccffcc"];
    verify_green [label="Verify passes\nAll green", shape=diamond];
    refactor [label="REFACTOR\nClean up", shape=box, style=filled, fillcolor="#ccccff"];
    next [label="Next", shape=ellipse];

    red -> verify_red;
    verify_red -> green [label="yes"];
    verify_red -> red [label="wrong\nfailure"];
    green -> verify_green;
    verify_green -> refactor [label="yes"];
    verify_green -> green [label="no"];
    refactor -> verify_green [label="stay\ngreen"];
    verify_green -> next;
    next -> red;
}
```

### RED - Write Failing Test

Write one minimal test showing what should happen.

+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;

+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{status:string}>(`SELECT e.status FROM agency_revenue_entries e JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be
+      ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 FOR UPDATE OF e`,[agency,id]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length||entries.rows.some(e=>e.status!=='AVAILABLE')) throw new ConflictError('Every batch entry must be AVAILABLE');
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_receipts (agency_id,batch_id,reference,created_by) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)`,[agency,id,reference,getUserId()]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries e SET status='RECEIVED',received_amount=be.amount,received_at=now(),received_reference=$3,updated_at=now()
+      FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 AND e.agency_id=be.agency_id AND e.id=be.entry_id`,[agency,id,reference]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_RECEIVED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'RECEIVED'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}

+import { describe,expect,it,vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { closeRevenueBatch,parseSettlementPolicy,settlementWindow,receiveRevenueBatch } from '../src/agency-revenue-batches';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../src/database';
+
+describe('revenue settlement windows',()=>{
+  it('clamps monthly closing days and adds real calendar days including leap years',()=>{
+    expect(settlementWindow('2024-02',31,5)).toEqual({start:'2024-01-31',end:'2024-02-29',expected:'2024-03-05'});
+    expect(settlementWindow('2026-01',15,20)).toEqual({start:'2025-12-15',end:'2026-01-15',expected:'2026-02-04'});
+  });
+  it.each(['2026-00','2026-13','x','0000-01'])('rejects invalid month %s',month=>expect(()=>settlementWindow(month,15,1)).toThrow());
+  it('requires an explicit calendar only for batch settlement',()=>{
+    expect(parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE'})).toEqual({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null});
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW'})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:31,paymentDays:-1})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',agencyId:'other'})).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('refuses writes for managers before opening a transaction',async()=>{
+    const withTenantTransaction=vi.fn();
+    const database={withTenantTransaction} as unknown as DatabaseRuntime;
+    await runWithTenantContext({agencyId:'a',userId:'u',userRole:UserRole.MANAGER,email:'u@example.test'},async()=>{
+      await expect(closeRevenueBatch(database,'2026-01')).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+      await expect(receiveRevenueBatch(database,'batch',null)).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+    });
+    expect(withTenantTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..e6a46d9c07f23758c27bd3140369dfa112069f17

@@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{status:string}>(`SELECT e.status FROM agency_revenue_entries e JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be
+      ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 FOR UPDATE OF e`,[agency,id]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length||entries.rows.some(e=>e.status!=='AVAILABLE')) throw new ConflictError('Every batch entry must be AVAILABLE');
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_receipts (agency_id,batch_id,reference,created_by) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)`,[agency,id,reference,getUserId()]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries e SET status='RECEIVED',received_amount=be.amount,received_at=now(),received_reference=$3,updated_at=now()
+      FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 AND e.agency_id=be.agency_id AND e.id=be.entry_id`,[agency,id,reference]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_RECEIVED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'RECEIVED'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}

+import type {FormSnapshot} from './sale-form-fields';
+
+type ImportPendingField = 'category'|'supplier'|'destination';
+
+export function buildImportFormSnapshot(pending: readonly ImportPendingField[]): FormSnapshot {
+  return {
+    categoryId:'',categoryName:'Importação histórica',formRevision:0,capturedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
+    importPending:[...pending],
+    // Destination remains editable through the existing typed form endpoint.
+    fields:[{fieldKey:'destination_text',label:'Destino',enabled:true,required:false}],
+  };
+}
+
+/** Requirements are historical, their missing state is always derived from the live sale. */
+export function remainingImportFields(snapshot:FormSnapshot|null,categoryId:string|null,destination:string|null,hasSupplier:boolean):string[] {
+  return (snapshot?.importPending??[]).flatMap(field=>{
+    if(field==='category'&&!categoryId) return ['category_id'];
+    if(field==='destination'&&!destination?.trim()) return ['destination_text'];
+    if(field==='supplier'&&!hasSupplier) return ['supplier_id'];
+    return [];
+  });
+}

+import { describe,expect,it,vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';

+describe('revenue settlement windows',()=>{
+  it('clamps monthly closing days and adds real calendar days including leap years',()=>{
+    expect(settlementWindow('2024-02',31,5)).toEqual({start:'2024-01-31',end:'2024-02-29',expected:'2024-03-05'});
+    expect(settlementWindow('2026-01',15,20)).toEqual({start:'2025-12-15',end:'2026-01-15',expected:'2026-02-04'});
+  });
+  it.each(['2026-00','2026-13','x','0000-01'])('rejects invalid month %s',month=>expect(()=>settlementWindow(month,15,1)).toThrow());
+  it('requires an explicit calendar only for batch settlement',()=>{
+    expect(parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE'})).toEqual({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null});
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW'})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:31,paymentDays:-1})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',agencyId:'other'})).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('refuses writes for managers before opening a transaction',async()=>{
+    const withTenantTransaction=vi.fn();
+    const database={withTenantTransaction} as unknown as DatabaseRuntime;
+    await runWithTenantContext({agencyId:'a',userId:'u',userRole:UserRole.MANAGER,email:'u@example.test'},async()=>{
+      await expect(closeRevenueBatch(database,'2026-01')).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+      await expect(receiveRevenueBatch(database,'batch',null)).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+    });
+    expect(withTenantTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

/**
 * Report projections over the agency's own data (Frente 9). Every query is
 * scoped by the session's agency inside withTenantTransaction (RLS on top);
 * filters are parameterised. The arithmetic lives in report-projections.ts.
 */
import { getAgencyId, getTenantContext, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import { requireRoleOrAreaGrant } from './area-grants';
import { computeEntryRelease } from './commission-release-service';
import { assertNotRestricted } from './permission-restrictions';

import { ValidationError } from './errors';
import {
  projectCommissions,
  projectReceivables,
  projectRevenue,
  supplierBalances,
  type CommissionRow,
  type ReceivableRow,
  type RevenueRow,
