export const PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT = 5000;

export interface ProjectionFilters {
  saleFrom?: string;
  saleTo?: string;
  travelFrom?: string;
  travelTo?: string;
  origin?: string;
  destination?: string;
  categoryId?: string;
  supplierId?: string;
  employeeId?: string;
  productId?: string;
  status?: string;
}

const DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const SALE_STATUSES = ['PENDING', 'CONFIRMED', 'PAID', 'CANCELLED'];

const COMMON_FILTERS = ['saleFrom', 'saleTo', 'travelFrom', 'travelTo', 'origin', 'destination', 'categoryId', 'status', 'format', 'supplierId', 'productId', 'employeeId'];
const ITEM_FILTERS = [...COMMON_FILTERS, 'supplierId', 'productId'];

/** `itemLevel` reports (agency revenue) also honour supplier/product; the others refuse them (never ignore silently). */
export function parseProjectionFilters(query: Record<string, unknown>, _itemLevel = false): ProjectionFilters {
  const allowed = ITEM_FILTERS;
  for (const key of Object.keys(query)) if (!allowed.includes(key)) throw new ValidationError(`Unknown query parameter "${key}"`);
  const text = (name: string, max: number): string | undefined => {
    const value = query[name];
    if (value === undefined || value === '') return undefined;
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > max) throw new ValidationError(`Query "${name}" is invalid`);
    return value;
  };
  const date = (name: string): string | undefined => {
    const value = text(name, 10);
    if (value !== undefined && (!DATE.test(value) || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) || new Date(value).toISOString().slice(0,10)!==value)) throw new ValidationError(`Query "${name}" must be YYYY-MM-DD`);
    return value;
  };
  const status = text('status', 20);
  if (status !== undefined && !SALE_STATUSES.includes(status)) throw new ValidationError('Query "status" is invalid');
  const filters: ProjectionFilters = {};
  const assign = <K extends keyof ProjectionFilters>(key: K, value: ProjectionFilters[K]) => {
    if (value !== undefined) filters[key] = value;
  };
  assign('saleFrom', date('saleFrom'));
  assign('saleTo', date('saleTo'));
  assign('travelFrom', date('travelFrom'));
  assign('travelTo', date('travelTo'));
  assign('origin', text('origin', 100));
  assign('destination', text('destination', 100));
  assign('categoryId', text('categoryId', 64));
  assign('supplierId', text('supplierId', 64));
  assign('employeeId', text('employeeId', 64));
  assign('productId', text('productId', 64));
  assign('status', status);
  if ((filters.saleFrom && filters.saleTo && filters.saleFrom>filters.saleTo) ||
      (filters.travelFrom && filters.travelTo && filters.travelFrom>filters.travelTo)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid date range');
  return filters;
}

/** WHERE fragment on `sales s` (always agency-scoped, always parameterised). */
function saleWhere(filters: ProjectionFilters, params: unknown[]): string {
  const conditions = [`s.agency_id = $1`];
  const add = (sql: (placeholder: string) => string, value: unknown) => {
    params.push(value);
    conditions.push(sql(`$${params.length}`));
  };
  if (filters.saleFrom) add((p) => `s.sale_date >= ${p}::date`, filters.saleFrom);
  if (filters.saleTo) add((p) => `s.sale_date <= ${p}::date`, filters.saleTo);
  if (filters.travelFrom) add((p) => `s.travel_start_date >= ${p}::date`, filters.travelFrom);
  if (filters.travelTo) add((p) => `s.travel_start_date <= ${p}::date`, filters.travelTo);
  if (filters.origin) add((p) => `s.origin_text ILIKE ${p}`, `%${escapeLike(filters.origin)}%`);
  if (filters.destination) add((p) => `s.destination_text ILIKE ${p}`, `%${escapeLike(filters.destination)}%`);
  if (filters.categoryId) add((p) => `s.category_id = ${p}`, filters.categoryId);
  if (filters.supplierId || filters.productId) {
    const itemConditions = ['fi.agency_id=s.agency_id','fi.sale_id=s.id',"fi.status='ACTIVE'"];
    if (filters.supplierId) { params.push(filters.supplierId); itemConditions.push('fi.supplier_id=((p) => `s.status::text = ${p}`, filters.status);
  else conditions.push(`s.status::text <> 'CANCELLED'`);
  return conditions.join(' AND ');
}

const escapeLike = (value: string): string => value.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (match) => `\\${match}`);

export const todayCivil = (): string => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo' }).format(new Date());

export async function getReceivablesProjection(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  // Same guard as the source receivables endpoint (role floor / area grant, minus restrictions and REVOKE).
  await requireRoleOrAreaGrant(database, UserRole.MANAGER, 'FINANCIAL_RECEIVABLES');
  const params: unknown[] = [getAgencyId()];
  const where = saleWhere(filters, params);
  const rows = await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<{ id: string; sale_id: string | null; due: string; amount: string; paid: string; status: string }>(
      `SELECT r.id, r.sale_id,
              COALESCE(r.due_date::text, to_char(r.due_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'YYYY-MM-DD')) AS due,
              r.amount::text AS amount, COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.direction='IN' AND NOT EXISTS (
                SELECT 1 FROM sale_payment_reversals spr WHERE spr.agency_id=p.agency_id AND spr.original_payment_id=p.id)), 0)::text AS paid, r.status::text AS status
         FROM receivables r
         JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = r.agency_id AND s.id = r.sale_id
         LEFT JOIN payment_allocations pa ON pa.agency_id = r.agency_id AND pa.receivable_id = r.id
         LEFT JOIN payments p ON p.agency_id=pa.agency_id AND p.id=pa.payment_id
        WHERE ${where} AND r.agency_id = $1
        GROUP BY r.id
        ORDER BY due, r.id
        LIMIT ${PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    return result.rows;
  });

/** Employee commission: forecast x released by customer receipts x paid (the revenue is never mixed in). */
/** One lookup per entry (the release state is computed per entry), so this report has a lower ceiling. */
export const COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT = 1000;

export async function getCommissionsProjection(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  const restrictionContext = getTenantContext();
  // Same restriction as the source employee-commission report.
  await database.withTenantTransaction((client) =>
    assertNotRestricted(client, restrictionContext.userRole, 'employees', 'view'),
  );
  const params: unknown[] = [getAgencyId()];
  const where = saleWhere(filters, params);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const entries = await client.query<{ id: string; amount: string; status: string }>(
      `SELECT ce.id, ce.amount::text AS amount, ce.status
         FROM commission_entries ce JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = ce.agency_id AND s.id = ce.sale_id
        WHERE ${where} AND ce.agency_id = $1
        ORDER BY ce.created_at, ce.id LIMIT ${COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    if (entries.rows.length > COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT) throw new ValidationError('Too many rows for this report; narrow the filters');
    const rows: CommissionRow[] = [];
    for (const entry of entries.rows) {
      const release = await computeEntryRelease(client, getAgencyId(), entry.id);
      rows.push({ entryId: entry.id, amount: Number(entry.amount), released: release.released, paid: release.paid, status: entry.status });
    }
    return projectCommissions(rows);
  });
}

export interface UpdateTaskInput {

export async function updateTask(id: string, input: UpdateTaskInput): Promise<CommercialTask> {
  const data = await request<{ task: CommercialTask }>(`/api/commercial/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {

  | **Direito** | a receita passou a ser devida | `ENTITLED` (com `entitled_at`) |

---
name: frontend-design
description: Guidance for distinctive, intentional visual design when building new UI or reshaping an existing one. Helps with aesthetic direction, typography, and making choices that don't read as templated defaults.
license: Complete terms in LICENSE.txt
---

# Frontend Design

Approach this as the design lead at a small studio known for giving every client a visual identity that could not be mistaken for anyone else's. This client has already rejected proposals that felt templated, and is paying for a distinctive point of view: make deliberate, opinionated choices about palette, typography, and layout that are specific to this brief, and take one real aesthetic risk you can justify.

## Ground it in the subject

If the brief does not pin down what the product or subject is, pin it yourself before designing: name one concrete subject, its audience, and the page's single job, and state your choice. If there's any information in your memory about the human's preferences, context about what they're building, or designs you've made before – use that as a hint. The subject's own world, its materials, instruments, artifacts, and vernacular, is where distinctive choices come from. Build with the brief's real content and subject matter throughout.

## Design principles

For web designs, the hero is a thesis. Open with the most characteristic thing in the subject's world, in whatever form makes sense for it: a headline, an image, an animation, a live demo, an interactive moment. Be deliberate with your choice: a big number with a small label, supporting stats, and a gradient accent is the template answer, only use if that's truly the best option.

Typography carries the personality of the page. Pair the display and body faces deliberately, not the same families you would reach for on any other project, and set a clear type scale with intentional weights, widths, and spacing. Make the type treatment itself a memorable part of the design, not a neutral delivery vehicle for the content.

Structure is information. Structural devices, numbering, eyebrows, dividers, labels, should encode something true about the content, not decorate it. Many generic designs use numbered markers (01 / 02 / 03), but that's only appropriate if the content actually is a sequence - like a real process or a typed timeline where order carries information the reader needs. Question if choices like numbered markers actually make sense before incorporating them.

Leverage motion deliberately. Think about where and if animation can serve the subject: a page-load sequence, a scroll-triggered reveal, hover micro-interactions, ambient atmosphere. An orchestrated moment usually lands harder than scattered effects; choose what the direction calls for. However, sometimes less is more, and extra animation contributes to the feeling that the design is AI-generated.

Match complexity to the vision. Maximalist directions need elaborate execution; minimal directions need precision in spacing, type, and detail. Elegance is executing the chosen vision well.

Consider written content carefully. Often a design brief may not contain real content, and it's up to you to come up with copy. Copy can make a design feel as templated as the design itself. See the below section on writing for more guidance.

## Process: brainstorm, explore, plan, critique, build, critique again

For calibration: AI-generated design right now clusters around three looks: (1) a warm cream background (near #F4F1EA) with a high-contrast serif display and a terracotta accent; (2) a near-black background with a single bright acid-green or vermilion accent; (3) a broadsheet-style layout with hairline rules, zero border-radius, and dense newspaper-like columns. All three are legitimate for some briefs, but they are defaults rather than choices, and they appear regardless of subject. Where the brief pins down a visual direction, follow it exactly — the brief's own words always win, including when it asks for one of these looks. Where it leaves an axis free, don't spend that freedom on one of these defaults. Just like a human designer who's hired, there's often a careful balance between doing what you're good at and taking each project as a chance to experiment and learn.

Work in two passes. First, brainstorm a short design plan based on the human's design brief: create a compact token system with color, type, layout, and signature. Color: describe the palette as 4–6 named hex values. Type: the typefaces for 2+ roles (a characterful display face that's used with restraint, a complementary body face, and a utility face for captions or data if needed). Layout: a layout concept, using one-sentence prose descriptions and ASCII wireframes to ideate and compare. Signature: the single unique element this page will be remembered by that embodies the brief in an appropriate way.

Then review that plan against the brief before building: if any part of it reads like the generic default you would produce for any similar page (work through a similar prompt to see if you arrive somewhere similar) rather than a choice made for this specific brief — revise that part, say what you changed and why. Only after you've confirmed the relative uniqueness of your design plan should you start to write the code, following the revised plan exactly and deriving every color and type decision from it.

When writing the code, be careful of structuring your CSS selector specificities. It's easy to generate CSS classes that cancel each other out (especially with a type-based selector like .section and a element-based selector like .cta). This can happen often with paddings/margins between sections.

Try to do a lot of this planning and iteration in your thinking, and only show ideas to the user when you have higher confidence it'll delight them.

## Restraint and self-critique

Spend your boldness in one place. Let the signature element be the one memorable thing, keep everything around it quiet and disciplined, and cut any decoration that does not serve the brief. Not taking a risk can be a risk itself! Build to a quality floor without announcing it: responsive down to mobile, visible keyboard focus, reduced motion respected. Critique your own work as you build, taking screenshots if your environment supports it – a picture is worth 1000 tokens. Consider Chanel's advice: before leaving the house, take a look in the mirror and remove one accessory. Human creators have memory and always try to do something new, so if you have a space to quickly jot down notes about what you've tried, it can help you in future passes.

## More on writing in design

Words appear in a design for one reason: to make it easier to understand, and therefore easier to use. They are design material, not decoration. Bring the same intentionality to copy that you would bring to spacing and color. Before writing anything, ask what the design needs to say, and how it can best be said to help the person navigate the experience.

Write from the end user's side of the screen. Name things by what people control and recognize, never by how the system is built. A person manages notifications, not webhook config. Describe what something does in plain terms rather than selling it. Being specific is always better than being clever.

Use active voice as default. A control should say exactly what happens when it's used: "Save changes," not "Submit." An action keeps the same name through the whole flow, so the button that says "Publish" produces a toast that says "Published." The vocabulary of an interface is the signposting for someone navigating the product. Cohesion and consistency are how people learn their way around.

Treat failure and emptiness as moments for direction, not mood. Explain what went wrong and how to fix it, in the interface's voice rather than a person's. Errors don't apologize, and they are never vague about what happened. An empty screen is an invitation to act.

Keep the register conversational and tuned: plain verbs, sentence case, no filler, with tone matched to the brand and the audience. Let each element do exactly one job. A label labels, an example demonstrates, and nothing quietly does double duty.

>   com receita recebida), D7 (lotes e janelas). A nomenclatura segue a spec §14.
  - **Relacionados:** ADR-006 (cronograma e quitação do cliente), ADR-008
    (passageiros e campos de venda), DEC-007, ADR-005.
  - **Revisão Codex:** não ocorreu.
  
  ## Contexto
  
  - **Não existe receita da agência em nenhum dos produtos.** O Lite tem apenas
    `seller_commissions` (comissão do vendedor). O Full tem apenas
    `commission_entries` e `employee_commission_rules` (comissão do funcionário)
    (spec §8; `COMMISSION-ENGINE-SPEC.md` §1; `employee-commissions.ts`).
  - `TravelProduct.commissionPercent` no Full é um campo **armazenado e validado,
    mas não consumido** por nenhum cálculo (grep em `services/api/src`, spec §5).
    Trata-se de dado órfão.
  - `SaleItem.commission` no Full: tipo verificado, semântica de uso **não
    verificada** (spec §15).
  - A Gadotti é a agência que revende. Ela recebe **% sobre o total**, **% sobre
    parte** (taxas, tarifas), **valor fixo por passageiro** (ônibus intermunicipal),
    **só sobre taxas** (cruzeiro) e **vários fornecedores com percentuais
> ### 4. Cálculo por item e por fornecedor
  
  - Bases, por item (`SaleItem`):
    - `LINE_TOTAL` = qty × preço − desconto + taxas + fees;
    - `NET_FARE` = qty × preço − desconto;
    - `TAXES`, `FEES`, `TAXES_AND_FEES`;
    - `MARGIN` (definição pendente, Q4).
  - `receita_item = round_half_up(base_item × taxa / 100, 2)`;
    `receita_venda = Σ receita_item`.
  - Cada item gera uma linha em `agency_revenue_entries` com **snapshot completo**:
    versão, base, taxa, memória de cálculo e `calc_version`.
  - Fixo por passageiro usa `passenger_count` da venda ou do item (ADR-008).
  - **Item sem fornecedor ou sem regra aplicável** gera entrada `PENDING_RULE`:
    valor 0, fora de qualquer relatório de receita reconhecida.
  - Arredondamento (Gadotti D4): para a **receita da agência**, half-up
    decimal-safe por item, igual ao `roundMoney` do Lite. Para a **comissão do
    funcionário**, vale o arredondamento atual de cada produto (Gadotti F4, sem
    mudança).
  
> ### 5. Estados e transições
  
  - `FORECAST` → `ENTITLED` → `AVAILABLE` → `RECEIVED`, com `CANCELLED` possível
    antes de `RECEIVED`.
  - Transições só no servidor, por uma função única de domínio, com auditoria.
  - **Modelo de arrecadação (Gadotti D1)**: parâmetro `collection_model` por
    fornecedor ou produto. `AGENCY_COLLECTS` é o padrão proposto (igual ao Lite
    hoje). `SUPPLIER_COLLECTS` é o cliente pagando o fornecedor diretamente.
    Sem essa escolha não se calcula fluxo de caixa.
  
> ### 6. Cancelamento, estorno e vínculo com quitação
  
  - Cancelar entrada em `FORECAST`, `ENTITLED` ou `AVAILABLE` → `CANCELLED`, sem
    efeito financeiro.
  - Cancelar venda com entrada `RECEIVED` → **409** por padrão (Gadotti D6). Não há
    clawback automático.
  - Estorno de baixa do cliente que desfaz a quitação, antes da liquidação do
    fornecedor, devolve a entrada de `ENTITLED` para `FORECAST`.
> - Entrada em lote fechado (`AVAILABLE` congelada) não pode ser cancelada sem ajuste
    manual (fora da v1).
  
> ### 7. Lotes e janelas (Gadotti D7)
  
  - `settlement_policy` com `mode`:
    - `PER_SALE`: cada entrada `ENTITLED` vira `AVAILABLE` na hora;
    - `BATCH`: agrupa por fornecedor e janela (semanal, quinzenal, mensal ou
      intervalo configurável), com **corte por `entitled_at`** e pagamento previsto
      por `payout_day_offset`.
  - **Sem calendário fixo no produto e sem padrão.** Cada fornecedor escolhe a
>   política. Não há default (Gadotti D7).
  - `agency_revenue_batches(id, supplier_id, window_start, window_end,
    expected_amount, received_amount, status OPEN|CLOSED|RECEIVED, closed_by,
    received_at, legacy_ref)`.
  - Fechar o lote congela as entradas. O recebimento efetivo é registrado com
    alocação ao lote, valor, data, conta e referência. A diferença
    (`expected − received`) fica **visível e aberta**; não há baixa automática por
    perda.
  - Uma entrada pertence a **no máximo um lote ativo** (índice único parcial). Mesma
    lição do canal único da F3.
  
  ### 8. Comissão do funcionário (Gadotti D3)
  
  - Ambientes separados: telas, permissões, tabelas e relatórios próprios. No menu,
    "Comissões de funcionários" (existente) e "Receita da agência" (novo).
  - Vínculo opcional: `commission_entries.agency_revenue_entry_id` e
    `seller_commissions.agency_revenue_entry_id` (nulo permitido), **somente para
    análise** ("receita da venda − comissão do funcionário"). **Sem netting.**
  - A obrigação com o funcionário **não depende** de o fornecedor ter pago a agência
> - Requer decisões D1 e D7 que ainda não foram tomadas formalmente.
  
  **Testes previstos** (propostos, ainda não existem)
  
  - `agency-revenue-rules.test.ts`: precedência, vigência, versões no mesmo dia.
  - `agency-revenue-calc.test.ts`: casos R1–R7 como tabela, com módulo puro de
    cálculo e paridade Full × Lite.
  - `agency-revenue-lifecycle.test.ts`: transições válidas e inválidas,
    concorrência, cancelamento.
  - `revenue-batches.test.ts`: janelas, corte por `entitled_at`, congelamento,
    diferença, unicidade de lote.
  - `revenue-commission-link.test.ts`: vínculo opcional, sem alterar valores, sem
    cancelamento automático.
  - Isolamento de tenant e auditoria com rollback.
  
  Casos numéricos de referência (valores ilustrativos, não da Gadotti):
  
  | # | Cenário | Resultado |
  | --- | --- | --- |
> | **D7** (lotes e janelas) | Decisão humana | Padrão: sem calendário fixo, sem default, corte por `entitled_at`. | 
Padrão proposto. Não aprovado. |
  | **F2** (fonte de "exige recebimento") | Decisão humana **pendente** | **Bloqueia a liberação de comissão do 
funcionário por recebimento** (item 8). A spec distingue `requires_receipt` (comissão, existente) de 
`counts_as_settled_on_authorization` (direito de receita, proposto). Não se pode rotular a regra na UI enquanto F2 não 
for confirmada. A spec **não** destrava COM-03. | **Continua pendente.** Este ADR não a resolve. |
  | **F1** (catálogo de categorias e atribuição no Full) | Decisão tomada, implementação pendente | Necessário para o 
relatório de receita por vendedor. | Aceita; sem implementação. |
  | **F3** (canal único, cancelar com 409) | Decisão tomada | Aplicada ao item 6 e ao item 7 (canal único). | Aceita. |
  | **Q1** `SUPPLIER_COLLECTS` | Pergunta | Quando a entrada vira `ENTITLED`? Proposta da spec: "a agência só rastreia 
pagamento para fins de direito". Precisa de definição. | Aberta |
  | **Q2** Razão do recebimento do fornecedor | Pergunta técnica | Usar `payments` (IN) ou tabela nova de recebimento 
de receita? Spec diz "payments IN ou equivalente". | Aberta |
  | **Q3** Produto órfão `commissionPercent` | Pergunta de migração | Migrar para regra de escopo produto ou descartar 
após decisão? Não remover antes de consolidar. | Aberta |
  | **Q4** Definição de `MARGIN` | Pergunta | Base `MARGIN` = venda − custo do item? Só faz sentido em 
`AGENCY_COLLECTS`. | Aberta |
  | **Q5** Local do módulo puro | Pergunta técnica | Ver seção de Lite. | Aberta |
  | **Q6** Política de `ON_BALANCE_DEADLINE` com atraso | Pergunta (spec A6) | Saldo atrasado não vira `ENTITLED` sem 
quitação. Confirmar. | Aberta |
  
  **Não decidido por este ADR:** valores de percentual, fornecedores, lotes e
  calendário reais da Gadotti. Tudo isso é configuração do cliente, não produto.
  
  ## Riscos
  
  - **Dupla contagem** de receita e de comissão, ou de receita e de venda bruta.
    Mitigação: separação de grandezas (item 1) e teste de relatório derivado.
  - **Erro de base de cálculo** (ex.: sobre o total em vez de sobre as taxas).

+import {beforeEach,describe,expect,it,vi} from 'vitest';
+import {fireEvent,render,screen,waitFor} from '@testing-library/react';
+import {RevenueBatchesPanel} from './RevenueBatchesPanel';
+import {api} from '../../lib/api';
+
+vi.mock('../../lib/api',()=>({api:{get:vi.fn(),put:vi.fn(),post:vi.fn()}}));
+beforeEach(()=>{
+  vi.clearAllMocks();
+  vi.mocked(api.get).mockImplementation(path=>Promise.resolve({data:path.endsWith('settlement-policy')?
+    {settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:25,paymentDays:7}:
+    {batches:[{id:'b1',month:'2026-09',expected_payment_date:'2026-10-02',amount:70,count:1,received_at:null}]}}));
+  vi.mocked(api.post).mockResolvedValue({data:{id:'b1'}});
+  vi.mocked(api.put).mockResolvedValue({data:{}});
+});
+describe('RevenueBatchesPanel',()=>{
+  it('saves an explicit window, closes the chosen month and receives its batch',async()=>{
+    render(<RevenueBatchesPanel/>);
+    expect(await screen.findByDisplayValue('25')).toBeInTheDocument();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Salvar política de recebimento'}));
+    await waitFor(()=>expect(api.put).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy',{settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:25,paymentDays:7}));
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Mês da janela'),{target:{value:'2026-09'}});
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Fechar lote'}));
+    await waitFor(()=>expect(api.post).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/agency-revenue/batches/close',{month:'2026-09'}));
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Referência do recebimento do lote'),{target:{value:'Extrato 10'}});
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Registrar recebimento do lote 2026-09'}));
+    await waitFor(()=>expect(api.post).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/agency-revenue/batches/b1/receive',{reference:'Extrato 10'}));
+  });
+  it('reports a failed load and does not offer mutation',async()=>{
+    vi.mocked(api.get).mockRejectedValue(new Error('Sem permissão'));
+    render(<RevenueBatchesPanel/>);
+    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Sem permissão');
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('button',{name:'Fechar lote'})).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+  });
+});

+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{status:string}>(`SELECT e.status FROM agency_revenue_entries e JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be
+      ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 FOR UPDATE OF e`,[agency,id]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length||entries.rows.some(e=>e.status!=='AVAILABLE')) throw new ConflictError('Every batch entry must be AVAILABLE');
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_receipts (agency_id,batch_id,reference,created_by) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)`,[agency,id,reference,getUserId()]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries e SET status='RECEIVED',received_amount=be.amount,received_at=now(),received_reference=$3,updated_at=now()
+      FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 AND e.agency_id=be.agency_id AND e.id=be.entry_id`,[agency,id,reference]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_RECEIVED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'RECEIVED'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+import type {FormSnapshot} from './sale-form-fields';
+
+type ImportPendingField = 'category'|'supplier'|'destination';
+
+export function buildImportFormSnapshot(pending: readonly ImportPendingField[]): FormSnapshot {
+  return {
+    categoryId:'',categoryName:'Importação histórica',formRevision:0,capturedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
+    importPending:[...pending],
+    // Destination remains editable through the existing typed form endpoint.
+    fields:[{fieldKey:'destination_text',label:'Destino',enabled:true,required:false}],
+  };
+}
+
+/** Requirements are historical, their missing state is always derived from the live sale. */
+export function remainingImportFields(snapshot:FormSnapshot|null,categoryId:string|null,destination:string|null,hasSupplier:boolean):string[] {
+  return (snapshot?.importPending??[]).flatMap(field=>{
+    if(field==='category'&&!categoryId) return ['category_id'];
+    if(field==='destination'&&!destination?.trim()) return ['destination_text'];
+    if(field==='supplier'&&!hasSupplier) return ['supplier_id'];
+    return [];
+  });
+}

+import { describe,expect,it,vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { closeRevenueBatch,parseSettlementPolicy,settlementWindow,receiveRevenueBatch } from '../src/agency-revenue-batches';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../src/database';
+
+describe('revenue settlement windows',()=>{
+  it('clamps monthly closing days and adds real calendar days including leap years',()=>{
+    expect(settlementWindow('2024-02',31,5)).toEqual({start:'2024-01-31',end:'2024-02-29',expected:'2024-03-05'});
+    expect(settlementWindow('2026-01',15,20)).toEqual({start:'2025-12-15',end:'2026-01-15',expected:'2026-02-04'});
+  });
+  it.each(['2026-00','2026-13','x','0000-01'])('rejects invalid month %s',month=>expect(()=>settlementWindow(month,15,1)).toThrow());
+  it('requires an explicit calendar only for batch settlement',()=>{
+    expect(parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE'})).toEqual({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null});
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW'})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:31,paymentDays:-1})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',agencyId:'other'})).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('refuses writes for managers before opening a transaction',async()=>{
+    const withTenantTransaction=vi.fn();
+    const database={withTenantTransaction} as unknown as DatabaseRuntime;
+    await runWithTenantContext({agencyId:'a',userId:'u',userRole:UserRole.MANAGER,email:'u@example.test'},async()=>{
+      await expect(closeRevenueBatch(database,'2026-01')).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+      await expect(receiveRevenueBatch(database,'batch',null)).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+    });
+    expect(withTenantTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

+import {beforeEach,describe,expect,it,vi} from 'vitest';
+import {fireEvent,render,screen,waitFor} from '@testing-library/react';
+import {RevenueBatchesPanel} from './RevenueBatchesPanel';
+import {api} from '../../lib/api';
+

+    {settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:25,paymentDays:7}:
+    {batches:[{id:'b1',month:'2026-09',expected_payment_date:'2026-10-02',amount:70,count:1,received_at:null}]}}));

+  vi.mocked(api.put).mockResolvedValue({data:{}});

+describe('RevenueBatchesPanel',()=>{

+    render(<RevenueBatchesPanel/>);
+    expect(await screen.findByDisplayValue('25')).toBeInTheDocument();
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Salvar política de recebimento'}));
+    await waitFor(()=>expect(api.put).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy',{settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:25,paymentDays:7}));
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Mês da janela'),{target:{value:'2026-09'}});
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Fechar lote'}));
+    await waitFor(()=>expect(api.post).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/agency-revenue/batches/close',{month:'2026-09'}));
+    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Referência do recebimento do lote'),{target:{value:'Extrato 10'}});
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Registrar recebimento do lote 2026-09'}));
+    await waitFor(()=>expect(api.post).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/agency-revenue/batches/b1/receive',{reference:'Extrato 10'}));
+  });
+  it('reports a failed load and does not offer mutation',async()=>{
+    vi.mocked(api.get).mockRejectedValue(new Error('Sem permissão'));
+    render(<RevenueBatchesPanel/>);
+    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Sem permissão');
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('button',{name:'Fechar lote'})).not.toBeInTheDocument();
+  });
+});

+import {useCallback,useEffect,useState} from 'react';
+import {Card,CardContent,CardHeader,CardTitle} from '../ui/card';
+import {api} from '../../lib/api';
+import {formatBRL} from '../../lib/formatCurrency';
+
+interface Policy {settlementMode:'PER_SALE'|'BATCH_WINDOW';closingDay:number|null;paymentDays:number|null}
+interface Batch {id:string;month:string;expected_payment_date:string;amount:number;count:number;received_at:string|null}
+
+export function RevenueBatchesPanel() {
+  const [policy,setPolicy]=useState<Policy|null>(null);
+  const [batches,setBatches]=useState<Batch[]>([]);
+  const [month,setMonth]=useState('');
+  const [reference,setReference]=useState('');
+  const [error,setError]=useState<string|null>(null);
+  const [notice,setNotice]=useState('');
+  const [busy,setBusy]=useState(false);
+  const load=useCallback(async()=>{
+    const [p,b]=await Promise.all([api.get<Policy>('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy'),api.get<{batches:Batch[]}>('/agency-revenue/batches')]);
+    setPolicy(p.data);setBatches(b.data.batches);
+  },[]);
+  useEffect(()=>{void load().catch(e=>setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Falha ao carregar lotes'));},[load]);
+  async function act(operation:()=>Promise<unknown>,message:string) {
+    setBusy(true);setError(null);setNotice('');
+    try {await operation();await load();setNotice(message);}
+    catch(e) {setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Não foi possível concluir a operação');}
+    finally {setBusy(false);}
+  }
+  return <Card><CardHeader><CardTitle>Lotes de receita</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent className="space-y-4">
+    {error&&<p role="alert">{error}</p>}{notice&&<p role="status">{notice}</p>}
+    {policy&&<>
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
+        <label>Recebimento<select className="block rounded border p-2" value={policy.settlementMode} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,settlementMode:e.target.value as Policy['settlementMode']})}><option value="PER_SALE">Por venda</option><option value="BATCH_WINDOW">Lotes por janela mensal</option></select></label>
+        {policy.settlementMode==='BATCH_WINDOW'&&<>
+          <label>Dia de fechamento<input className="block w-28 rounded border p-2" type="number" min="1" max="31" value={policy.closingDay??''} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,closingDay:e.target.value===''?null:Number(e.target.value)})}/></label>
+          <label>Dias até pagamento<input className="block w-28 rounded border p-2" type="number" min="0" max="365" value={policy.paymentDays??''} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,paymentDays:e.target.value===''?null:Number(e.target.value)})}/></label>
+        </>}
+        <button className="rounded border px-3 py-2" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.put('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy',policy),'Política salva.')}>Salvar política de recebimento</button>
+      </div>
+      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Janela: após o fechamento anterior até o dia de fechamento, inclusive. Dias 29–31 usam o último dia disponível do mês. Feche após o término desse dia.</p>
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3"><label>Mês da janela<input className="block rounded border p-2" type="month" value={month} onChange={e=>setMonth(e.target.value)}/></label><button className="rounded border px-3 py-2" disabled={busy||!month||policy.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW'} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.post('/agency-revenue/batches/close',{month}),'Lote fechado.')}>Fechar lote</button></div>
+      <label className="block">Referência do recebimento do lote<input className="block w-full rounded border p-2" maxLength={200} value={reference} onChange={e=>setReference(e.target.value)}/></label>
+      <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left text-sm"><thead><tr><th>Janela</th><th>Pagamento previsto</th><th>Entradas</th><th>Valor</th><th>Recebimento</th></tr></thead><tbody>{batches.map(b=><tr key={b.id} className="border-t"><td>{b.month}</td><td>{b.expected_payment_date}</td><td>{b.count}</td><td>{formatBRL(b.amount)}</td><td>{b.received_at?'Recebido':<button className="my-2 rounded border p-2" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.post(`/agency-revenue/batches/${encodeURIComponent(b.id)}/receive`,{reference}),'Recebimento registrado.')}>Registrar recebimento do lote {b.month}</button>}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
+      {!batches.length&&<p>Nenhum lote fechado.</p>}
+    </>}
+  </CardContent></Card>;
+}

+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{status:string}>(`SELECT e.status FROM agency_revenue_entries e JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be
+      ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 FOR UPDATE OF e`,[agency,id]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length||entries.rows.some(e=>e.status!=='AVAILABLE')) throw new ConflictError('Every batch entry must be AVAILABLE');
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_receipts (agency_id,batch_id,reference,created_by) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)`,[agency,id,reference,getUserId()]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries e SET status='RECEIVED',received_amount=be.amount,received_at=now(),received_reference=$3,updated_at=now()
+      FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 AND e.agency_id=be.agency_id AND e.id=be.entry_id`,[agency,id,reference]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_RECEIVED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'RECEIVED'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}

+import type {FormSnapshot} from './sale-form-fields';
+
+type ImportPendingField = 'category'|'supplier'|'destination';
+
+export function buildImportFormSnapshot(pending: readonly ImportPendingField[]): FormSnapshot {
+  return {
+    categoryId:'',categoryName:'Importação histórica',formRevision:0,capturedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
+    importPending:[...pending],
+    // Destination remains editable through the existing typed form endpoint.
+    fields:[{fieldKey:'destination_text',label:'Destino',enabled:true,required:false}],
+  };
+}
+
+/** Requirements are historical, their missing state is always derived from the live sale. */
+export function remainingImportFields(snapshot:FormSnapshot|null,categoryId:string|null,destination:string|null,hasSupplier:boolean):string[] {
+  return (snapshot?.importPending??[]).flatMap(field=>{
+    if(field==='category'&&!categoryId) return ['category_id'];
+    if(field==='destination'&&!destination?.trim()) return ['destination_text'];
+    if(field==='supplier'&&!hasSupplier) return ['supplier_id'];
+    return [];
+  });
+}

+import { describe,expect,it,vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { closeRevenueBatch,parseSettlementPolicy,settlementWindow,receiveRevenueBatch } from '../src/agency-revenue-batches';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../src/database';
+
+describe('revenue settlement windows',()=>{
+  it('clamps monthly closing days and adds real calendar days including leap years',()=>{
+    expect(settlementWindow('2024-02',31,5)).toEqual({start:'2024-01-31',end:'2024-02-29',expected:'2024-03-05'});
+    expect(settlementWindow('2026-01',15,20)).toEqual({start:'2025-12-15',end:'2026-01-15',expected:'2026-02-04'});
+  });
+  it.each(['2026-00','2026-13','x','0000-01'])('rejects invalid month %s',month=>expect(()=>settlementWindow(month,15,1)).toThrow());
+  it('requires an explicit calendar only for batch settlement',()=>{
+    expect(parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE'})).toEqual({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null});
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW'})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:31,paymentDays:-1})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',agencyId:'other'})).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('refuses writes for managers before opening a transaction',async()=>{
+    const withTenantTransaction=vi.fn();
+    const database={withTenantTransaction} as unknown as DatabaseRuntime;
+    await runWithTenantContext({agencyId:'a',userId:'u',userRole:UserRole.MANAGER,email:'u@example.test'},async()=>{
+      await expect(closeRevenueBatch(database,'2026-01')).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+      await expect(receiveRevenueBatch(database,'batch',null)).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+    });
+    expect(withTenantTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

+import {useEffect,useState,type ReactNode} from 'react';
+import {api,type DashboardSummary} from '../lib/api';
+import {Card,CardContent,CardHeader,CardTitle} from './ui/card';
+import {LoadingState} from './ui/loading-state';
+import {formatBRL} from '../lib/formatCurrency';
+
+interface Widget {key:string;title:string;data:Record<string,unknown>}
+const object=(value:unknown):Record<string,unknown>=>value!==null&&typeof value==='object'&&!Array.isArray(value)?value as Record<string,unknown>:{};
+const amount=(value:unknown)=>typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='string'?formatBRL(Number(value)):'—';
+
+function widgetRows(widget:Widget):Array<[string,string]> {
+  const d=widget.data;
+  switch(widget.key) {
+    case 'financial_overview': return [['Saldo previsto',amount(object(d.projected).balance)],['Entradas realizadas',amount(object(d.realized).paymentsIn)],['Saídas realizadas',amount(object(d.realized).paymentsOut)],['Saldo realizado',amount(object(d.realized).balance)]];
+    case 'receivables_projection': return [['Atrasado',amount(object(d.late).openBalance)],['Total em aberto',amount(d.totalOpen)]];
+    case 'revenue_forecast': return [['Contratada',amount(object(d.contracted).total)],['Projetada',amount(object(d.projected).total)],['Sem data',amount(object(d.undefined).total)]];
+    case 'commissions_projection': return [['Prevista',amount(d.forecast)],['Liberada',amount(d.released)],['Paga',amount(d.paid)],['Liberada e não paga',amount(d.releasedNotPaid)]];
+    case 'supplier_balances': return [];
+    default:return [];
+  }
+}
+
+/** The response is already ordered and permission-filtered by the server. No client catalog restores omitted widgets. */
+export function DashboardWidgets({commercial}:{commercial:(summary:DashboardSummary)=>ReactNode}) {
+  const [widgets,setWidgets]=useState<Widget[]|null>(null);
+  const [error,setError]=useState<string|null>(null);
+  useEffect(()=>{
+    let active=true;
+    api.get<{widgets:Widget[]}>('/dashboard/widgets').then(({data})=>{if(active)setWidgets(data.widgets);}).catch((e:unknown)=>{if(active)setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Não foi possível carregar o painel.');});
+    return ()=>{active=false;};
+  },[]);
+  if(error) return <p role="alert">{error}</p>;
+  if(!widgets) return <LoadingState label="Carregando resumo da agência…"/>;
+  return <div className="space-y-6">{!widgets.length&&<p>Nenhum widget habilitado para este usuário.</p>}{widgets.map(w=>{
+    const unavailable=w.data?.unavailable;
+    if(w.key==='commercial_overview'&&!unavailable) return <section key={w.key} aria-label={w.title}>{commercial(w.data as unknown as DashboardSummary)}</section>;
+    return <Card key={w.key}><CardHeader><CardTitle>{w.title}</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent>
+      {unavailable?<p>Refine os filtros nas projeções para consultar este volume de dados.</p>:w.key==='supplier_balances'?<>
+        {Array.isArray(w.data)&&w.data.length===0&&<p>Nenhum saldo com fornecedores.</p>}
+        {Array.isArray(w.data)&&w.data.map((raw:unknown,index:number)=>{const row=object(raw);return <p key={String(row.supplierId??index)}>{String(row.supplierId??'Sem fornecedor')}: esperado {amount(row.expected)} · recebido {amount(row.received)} · diferença {amount(row.difference)}</p>;})}
+      </>:<dl>{widgetRows(w).map(([label,value])=><div className="flex justify-between gap-4 py-1" key={label}><dt>{label}</dt><dd>{value}</dd></div>)}</dl>}
+    </CardContent></Card>;
+  })}</div>;
+}

+import {useCallback,useEffect,useState} from 'react';
+import {Card,CardContent,CardHeader,CardTitle} from '../ui/card';
+import {api} from '../../lib/api';
+import {formatBRL} from '../../lib/formatCurrency';
+
+interface Policy {settlementMode:'PER_SALE'|'BATCH_WINDOW';closingDay:number|null;paymentDays:number|null}
+interface Batch {id:string;month:string;expected_payment_date:string;amount:number;count:number;received_at:string|null}
+
+export function RevenueBatchesPanel() {
+  const [policy,setPolicy]=useState<Policy|null>(null);
+  const [batches,setBatches]=useState<Batch[]>([]);
+  const [month,setMonth]=useState('');
+  const [reference,setReference]=useState('');
+  const [error,setError]=useState<string|null>(null);
+  const [notice,setNotice]=useState('');
+  const [busy,setBusy]=useState(false);
+  const load=useCallback(async()=>{
+    const [p,b]=await Promise.all([api.get<Policy>('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy'),api.get<{batches:Batch[]}>('/agency-revenue/batches')]);
+    setPolicy(p.data);setBatches(b.data.batches);
+  },[]);
+  useEffect(()=>{void load().catch(e=>setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Falha ao carregar lotes'));},[load]);
+  async function act(operation:()=>Promise<unknown>,message:string) {
+    setBusy(true);setError(null);setNotice('');
+    try {await operation();await load();setNotice(message);}
+    catch(e) {setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Não foi possível concluir a operação');}
+    finally {setBusy(false);}
+  }
+  return <Card><CardHeader><CardTitle>Lotes de receita</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent className="space-y-4">
+    {error&&<p role="alert">{error}</p>}{notice&&<p role="status">{notice}</p>}
+    {policy&&<>
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
+        <label>Recebimento<select className="block rounded border p-2" value={policy.settlementMode} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,settlementMode:e.target.value as Policy['settlementMode']})}><option value="PER_SALE">Por venda</option><option value="BATCH_WINDOW">Lotes por janela mensal</option></select></label>
+        {policy.settlementMode==='BATCH_WINDOW'&&<>
+          <label>Dia de fechamento<input className="block w-28 rounded border p-2" type="number" min="1" max="31" value={policy.closingDay??''} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,closingDay:e.target.value===''?null:Number(e.target.value)})}/></label>
+          <label>Dias até pagamento<input className="block w-28 rounded border p-2" type="number" min="0" max="365" value={policy.paymentDays??''} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,paymentDays:e.target.value===''?null:Number(e.target.value)})}/></label>
+        </>}
+        <button className="rounded border px-3 py-2" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.put('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy',policy),'Política salva.')}>Salvar política de recebimento</button>
+      </div>
+      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Janela: após o fechamento anterior até o dia de fechamento, inclusive. Dias 29–31 usam o último dia disponível do mês. Feche após o término desse dia.</p>
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3"><label>Mês da janela<input className="block rounded border p-2" type="month" value={month} onChange={e=>setMonth(e.target.value)}/></label><button className="rounded border px-3 py-2" disabled={busy||!month||policy.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW'} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.post('/agency-revenue/batches/close',{month}),'Lote fechado.')}>Fechar lote</button></div>
+      <label className="block">Referência do recebimento do lote<input className="block w-full rounded border p-2" maxLength={200} value={reference} onChange={e=>setReference(e.target.value)}/></label>
+      <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left text-sm"><thead><tr><th>Janela</th><th>Pagamento previsto</th><th>Entradas</th><th>Valor</th><th>Recebimento</th></tr></thead><tbody>{batches.map(b=><tr key={b.id} className="border-t"><td>{b.month}</td><td>{b.expected_payment_date}</td><td>{b.count}</td><td>{formatBRL(b.amount)}</td><td>{b.received_at?'Recebido':<button className="my-2 rounded border p-2" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.post(`/agency-revenue/batches/${encodeURIComponent(b.id)}/receive`,{reference}),'Recebimento registrado.')}>Registrar recebimento do lote {b.month}</button>}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
+      {!batches.length&&<p>Nenhum lote fechado.</p>}
+    </>}
+  </CardContent></Card>;
+}

+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..87c7eb78381012d3c5c6896fd1e034d4a1f19869
