+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{status:string}>(`SELECT e.status FROM agency_revenue_entries e JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be
+      ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 FOR UPDATE OF e`,[agency,id]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length||entries.rows.some(e=>e.status!=='AVAILABLE')) throw new ConflictError('Every batch entry must be AVAILABLE');
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_receipts (agency_id,batch_id,reference,created_by) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)`,[agency,id,reference,getUserId()]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries e SET status='RECEIVED',received_amount=be.amount,received_at=now(),received_reference=$3,updated_at=now()
+      FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 AND e.agency_id=be.agency_id AND e.id=be.entry_id`,[agency,id,reference]);

diff --git a/services/api/src/sales-import-completion.ts b/services/api/src/sales-import-completion.ts

@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+import type {FormSnapshot} from './sale-form-fields';
+
+type ImportPendingField = 'category'|'supplier'|'destination';
+
+export function buildImportFormSnapshot(pending: readonly ImportPendingField[]): FormSnapshot {
+  return {
+    categoryId:'',categoryName:'Importação histórica',formRevision:0,capturedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
+    importPending:[...pending],
+    // Destination remains editable through the existing typed form endpoint.
+    fields:[{fieldKey:'destination_text',label:'Destino',enabled:true,required:false}],
+  };
+}
+
+/** Requirements are historical, their missing state is always derived from the live sale. */
+export function remainingImportFields(snapshot:FormSnapshot|null,categoryId:string|null,destination:string|null,hasSupplier:boolean):string[] {
+  return (snapshot?.importPending??[]).flatMap(field=>{
+    if(field==='category'&&!categoryId) return ['category_id'];
+    if(field==='destination'&&!destination?.trim()) return ['destination_text'];
+    if(field==='supplier'&&!hasSupplier) return ['supplier_id'];
+    return [];
+  });
+}

+import { describe,expect,it,vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { closeRevenueBatch,parseSettlementPolicy,settlementWindow,receiveRevenueBatch } from '../src/agency-revenue-batches';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../src/database';
+
+describe('revenue settlement windows',()=>{
+  it('clamps monthly closing days and adds real calendar days including leap years',()=>{
+    expect(settlementWindow('2024-02',31,5)).toEqual({start:'2024-01-31',end:'2024-02-29',expected:'2024-03-05'});
+    expect(settlementWindow('2026-01',15,20)).toEqual({start:'2025-12-15',end:'2026-01-15',expected:'2026-02-04'});
+  });
+  it.each(['2026-00','2026-13','x','0000-01'])('rejects invalid month %s',month=>expect(()=>settlementWindow(month,15,1)).toThrow());
+  it('requires an explicit calendar only for batch settlement',()=>{
+    expect(parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE'})).toEqual({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null});
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW'})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:31,paymentDays:-1})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',agencyId:'other'})).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('refuses writes for managers before opening a transaction',async()=>{
+    const withTenantTransaction=vi.fn();
+    const database={withTenantTransaction} as unknown as DatabaseRuntime;
+    await runWithTenantContext({agencyId:'a',userId:'u',userRole:UserRole.MANAGER,email:'u@example.test'},async()=>{
+      await expect(closeRevenueBatch(database,'2026-01')).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+      await expect(receiveRevenueBatch(database,'batch',null)).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+    });
+    expect(withTenantTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

  .filter((name) => /^\d+_.+\.sql$/.test(name))

describe('IMP-01 sales import over HTTP with a real database', () => {
  let db: DisposableDb;
  let admin: Pool;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await startDisposableDb(MIGRATIONS);
    admin = db.adminPool;
    await admin.query(
      `INSERT INTO agencies (id, name, slug, email, plan, status) VALUES
        ($1, 'Agency A', 'agency-a-imp', 'a@example.test', 'FREE', 'ACTIVE'),
        ($2, 'Agency B', 'agency-b-imp', 'b@example.test', 'FREE', 'ACTIVE')`,
      [agencyA, agencyB],
    );
    for (const [key, id] of Object.entries(ids)) {
      const [agencyId, role] = roleOf[key as keyof typeof ids];
      await admin.query(
        `INSERT INTO users (id, agency_id, email, name, role, password_hash, status) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'hash-test-only', 'ACTIVE')`,
        [id, agencyId, `${key}@example.test`, key, role],
      );
    }
    await admin.query(`INSERT INTO customers (agency_id, name, status, cpf) VALUES ($1, 'Maria', 'ACTIVE', '52998224725')`, [agencyA]);
    await admin.query(`INSERT INTO customers (agency_id, name, status, cpf) VALUES ($1, 'Outra Agência', 'ACTIVE', '39053344705')`, [agencyB]);
    await admin.query(`INSERT INTO suppliers (agency_id, name) VALUES ($1, 'Latam')`, [agencyA]);
    await admin.query(`INSERT INTO commission_categories (agency_id, name) VALUES ($1, 'Aéreo')`, [agencyA]).catch(async () => {
      await admin.query(`INSERT INTO commission_categories (agency_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Aéreo', 'ACTIVE')`, [agencyA]);
    });
  }, 300_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await db?.stop();
  });

  function app() {
    return buildApp({
      authProvider: {
        async authenticate(request) {
          const token = request.headers['x-test-user'];
          if (typeof token !== 'string') return null;
          const row = (await admin.query<{ id: string; agency_id: string; role: string; email: string }>(
            `SELECT id, agency_id, role, email FROM users WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'ACTIVE'`, [token])).rows[0];
          if (!row) return null;
          const principal: AuthenticatedPrincipal = { userId: row.id, agencyId: row.agency_id, role: row.role as UserRole, email: row.email };
          return principal;
        },
      },
      validateUserAgencyAccess: async (userId, agencyId) =>
        (await admin.query(`SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE id = $1 AND agency_id = $2`, [userId, agencyId])).rowCount === 1,
      database: createDatabaseRuntime(db.runtimePool),
    });
  }
  async function post(as: string | null, url: string, payload: unknown) {
    const server = app();
    try {
      const res = await server.inject({ method: 'POST', url, ...(as ? { headers: { 'x-test-user': as } } : {}), payload: payload as object });
      return { status: res.statusCode, body: res.json<Record<string, any>>() };
    } finally {
      await server.close();
    }
  }
  const dry = (as: string | null, body: unknown) => post(as, '/imports/sales/dry-run', body);
  const confirm = (as: string | null, body: unknown) => post(as, '/imports/sales/confirm', body);
  const count = async (table: string, agencyId = agencyA) => Number((await admin.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM ${table} WHERE agency_id = $1`, [agencyId])).rows[0].n);

  it('requires authentication and MANAGER+; validates the body', async () => {
    const body = { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: rows() };
    expect((await dry(null, body)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await dry(ids.agentA, body)).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await confirm(ids.agentA, { ...body, contentHash: 'a'.repeat(64) })).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await dry(ids.managerA, { ...body, agencyId: agencyB })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await dry(ids.managerA, { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: [{ external_ref: 'x', password: 'y' }] })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await dry(ids.managerA, { sourceSystem: 'A|B', rows: [] })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await confirm(ids.managerA, body)).status).toBe(400);
  });

  it('dry run writes nothing and reports new / rejected / a completar', async () => {
    const before = await count('sales');
    const res = await dry(ids.managerA, { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: rows() });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.plan.summary).toMatchObject({ total: 3, new: 2, rejected: 1, toComplete: 1 });
    expect(res.body.plan.rows[2].errors[0].column).toBe('customer_cpf');
    expect(res.body.plan.rows[1].toComplete).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['category', 'supplier', 'destination']));
    expect(await count('sales')).toBe(before);
    expect(await count('sale_import_batches')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('confirm requires the dry-run hash, creates the sales, never commissions or receivables, and audits', async () => {
    const body = { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: rows() };
    const plan = await dry(ids.managerA, body);
    expect((await confirm(ids.managerA, { ...body, contentHash: 'b'.repeat(64) })).status).toBe(409);
    const commissionsBefore = await count('commission_entries');
    const receivablesBefore = await count('receivables');
    const res = await confirm(ids.managerA, { ...body, contentHash: plan.body.contentHash });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ replayed: false, summary: { created: 2, rejected: 1, toComplete: 1 } });
    expect(res.body.summary.toCompleteRows).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(res.body.summary.rejections[0].row).toBe(3);
    expect(await count('sales')).toBe(2);
    expect(await count('sale_items')).toBe(1);
    expect(await count('commission_entries')).toBe(commissionsBefore);
    expect(await count('receivables')).toBe(receivablesBefore);
    const sale = (await admin.query(`SELECT total::float8 AS total, status::text AS status, sale_date::text AS sale_date, destination_text FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 ORDER BY total DESC`, [agencyA])).rows;
    expect(sale[0]).toMatchObject({ total: 1500.5, status: 'CONFIRMED', sale_date: '2026-09-10', destination_text: 'Gramado' });
    expect((await admin.query(`SELECT 1 FROM audit_logs WHERE agency_id = $1 AND event_type = 'SALES_IMPORTED'`, [agencyA])).rowCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('the same file again writes nothing (replay); a different file with the same sales creates no duplicates', async () => {
    const body = { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: rows() };
    const plan = await dry(ids.managerA, body);
    expect(plan.body.alreadyImported).toBe(true);
    const again = await confirm(ids.managerA, { ...body, contentHash: plan.body.contentHash });
    expect(again.body.replayed).toBe(true);
    expect(await count('sales')).toBe(2);

    const extra = { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: rows([{ external_ref: 'V-4', sale_date: '2026-09-13', customer_cpf: '529.982.247-25', total: '50' }]) };
    const plan2 = await dry(ids.managerA, extra);
    expect(plan2.body.plan.summary).toMatchObject({ new: 1, alreadyImported: 2, rejected: 1 });
    const res = await confirm(ids.managerA, { ...extra, contentHash: plan2.body.contentHash });
    expect(res.body.summary.created).toBe(1);
    expect(await count('sales')).toBe(3);
    expect(await count('sale_import_keys')).toBe(3);
  });

  it('the database itself refuses a duplicated natural key and a duplicated content hash', async () => {
    await expect(admin.query(
      `INSERT INTO sale_import_keys (agency_id, source_system, external_ref, sale_id, batch_id)
       SELECT agency_id, source_system, external_ref, sale_id, batch_id FROM sale_import_keys WHERE agency_id = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [agencyA],
    )).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(db.runtimePool.query('DELETE FROM sale_import_keys')).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(db.runtimePool.query('UPDATE sale_import_batches SET summary = \'{}\'')).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('is isolated by agency: B cannot see or match A\'s customers, and A\'s keys do not block B', async () => {
    const body = { sourceSystem: 'BWT', rows: [{ external_ref: 'V-1', sale_date: '2026-09-10', customer_cpf: '390.533.447-05', total: '10' }] };
    const plan = await dry(ids.managerB, body);
    expect(plan.body.alreadyImported).toBe(false);
    expect(plan.body.plan.summary).toMatchObject({ new: 1, alreadyImported: 0 });
      `SELECT r.id, r.sale_id,
              COALESCE(r.due_date::text, to_char(r.due_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'YYYY-MM-DD')) AS due,
              r.amount::text AS amount, COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.direction='IN' AND NOT EXISTS (
                SELECT 1 FROM sale_payment_reversals spr WHERE spr.agency_id=p.agency_id AND spr.original_payment_id=p.id)), 0)::text AS paid, r.status::text AS status
         FROM receivables r
         JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = r.agency_id AND s.id = r.sale_id
         LEFT JOIN payment_allocations pa ON pa.agency_id = r.agency_id AND pa.receivable_id = r.id
         LEFT JOIN payments p ON p.agency_id=pa.agency_id AND p.id=pa.payment_id
        WHERE ${where} AND r.agency_id = $1
        GROUP BY r.id
        ORDER BY due, r.id
        LIMIT ${PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    return result.rows;
  });
  if (rows.length > PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT) throw new ValidationError('Too many rows for this report; narrow the filters');
  const data: ReceivableRow[] = rows.map((row) => ({
    id: row.id, saleId: row.sale_id, dueDate: row.due, amount: Number(row.amount), paid: Number(row.paid), status: row.status,
  }));
  return projectReceivables(data, todayCivil());
}

async function loadRevenueRows(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters): Promise<RevenueRow[]> {
  const params: unknown[] = [getAgencyId()];
  const where = saleWhere(filters, params);
  const extra: string[] = [];
  if (filters.supplierId) {
    params.push(filters.supplierId);
    extra.push(`e.supplier_id = $${params.length}`);
  }
  if (filters.productId) {
    params.push(filters.productId);
    extra.push(`i.product_id = $${params.length}`);
  }
  const rows = await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<{
      id: string; supplier_id: string | null; status: string; amount: string; received_amount: string | null;
      entitled_on: string | null; expected_on: string | null;
    }>(
      `SELECT e.id, e.supplier_id, e.status, e.amount::text AS amount, e.received_amount::text AS received_amount,
              to_char(e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS entitled_on,
              CASE WHEN e.status = 'FORECAST' AND e.entitlement_event <> 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION' THEN
                (SELECT MAX(COALESCE(r.due_date::text, to_char(r.due_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'YYYY-MM-DD')))
                   FROM receivables r WHERE r.agency_id = e.agency_id AND r.sale_id = e.sale_id AND r.status::text <> 'CANCELLED')
              END AS expected_on
         FROM agency_revenue_entries e
         JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = e.agency_id AND s.id = e.sale_id
         JOIN sale_items i ON i.agency_id = e.agency_id AND i.id = e.sale_item_id
        WHERE ${where}${extra.length ? ` AND ${extra.join(' AND ')}` : ''} AND e.status <> 'CANCELLED'
        ORDER BY e.created_at, e.id
        LIMIT ${PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    return result.rows;
  });
  if (rows.length > PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT) throw new ValidationError('Too many rows for this report; narrow the filters');
  return rows.map((row) => ({
    entryId: row.id,
    supplierId: row.supplier_id,
    status: row.status,
    amount: Number(row.amount),
    receivedAmount: row.received_amount === null ? null : Number(row.received_amount),
    entitledOn: row.entitled_on,
    expectedOn: row.expected_on,
  }));
}

export async function getRevenueForecast(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return projectRevenue(await loadRevenueRows(database, filters));
}

export async function getSupplierBalances(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return supplierBalances(await loadRevenueRows(database, filters));
}

/** Employee commission: forecast x released by customer receipts x paid (the revenue is never mixed in). */
/** One lookup per entry (the release state is computed per entry), so this report has a lower ceiling. */
export const COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT = 1000;

export async function getCommissionsProjection(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  const restrictionContext = getTenantContext();
  // Same restriction as the source employee-commission report.
  await database.withTenantTransaction((client) =>
    assertNotRestricted(client, restrictionContext.userRole, 'employees', 'view'),
  );
  const params: unknown[] = [getAgencyId()];
  let where = saleWhere(filters, params);
  if(filters.employeeId) {params.push(filters.employeeId);where+=' AND ce.employee_id=$'+params.length;}
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const entries = await client.query<{ id: string; amount: string; status: string }>(
      `SELECT ce.id, ce.amount::text AS amount, ce.status
         FROM commission_entries ce JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = ce.agency_id AND s.id = ce.sale_id
        WHERE ${where} AND ce.agency_id = $1
        ORDER BY ce.created_at, ce.id LIMIT ${COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    if (entries.rows.length > COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT) throw new ValidationError('Too many rows for this report; narrow the filters');
    const rows: CommissionRow[] = [];
    for (const entry of entries.rows) {
      const release = await computeEntryRelease(client, getAgencyId(), entry.id);
      rows.push({ entryId: entry.id, amount: Number(entry.amount), released: release.released, paid: release.paid, status: entry.status });
    }
    return projectCommissions(rows);
  });
}

+import {useEffect,useState,type ReactNode} from 'react';

+interface Widget {key:string;title:string;data:Record<string,unknown>}
+const object=(value:unknown):Record<string,unknown>=>value!==null&&typeof value==='object'&&!Array.isArray(value)?value as Record<string,unknown>:{};
+const amount=(value:unknown)=>typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='string'?formatBRL(Number(value)):'—';
+
+function widgetRows(widget:Widget):Array<[string,string]> {
+  const d=widget.data;
+  switch(widget.key) {
+    case 'financial_overview': return [['Saldo previsto',amount(object(d.projected).balance)],['Entradas realizadas',amount(object(d.realized).paymentsIn)],['Saídas realizadas',amount(object(d.realized).paymentsOut)],['Saldo realizado',amount(object(d.realized).balance)]];
+    case 'receivables_projection': return [['Atrasado',amount(object(d.late).openBalance)],['Total em aberto',amount(d.totalOpen)]];
+    case 'revenue_forecast': return [['Contratada',amount(object(d.contracted).total)],['Projetada',amount(object(d.projected).total)],['Sem data',amount(object(d.undefined).total)]];
+    case 'commissions_projection': return [['Prevista',amount(d.forecast)],['Liberada',amount(d.released)],['Paga',amount(d.paid)],['Liberada e não paga',amount(d.releasedNotPaid)]];
+    case 'supplier_balances': return [];
+    default:return [];
+  }
+}

+import {useCallback,useEffect,useState} from 'react';
+import {Card,CardContent,CardHeader,CardTitle} from '../ui/card';
+import {api} from '../../lib/api';
+import {formatBRL} from '../../lib/formatCurrency';
+
+interface Policy {settlementMode:'PER_SALE'|'BATCH_WINDOW';closingDay:number|null;paymentDays:number|null}
+interface Batch {id:string;month:string;expected_payment_date:string;amount:number;count:number;received_at:string|null}
+
+export function RevenueBatchesPanel() {
+  const [policy,setPolicy]=useState<Policy|null>(null);
+  const [batches,setBatches]=useState<Batch[]>([]);
+  const [month,setMonth]=useState('');
+  const [reference,setReference]=useState('');
+  const [error,setError]=useState<string|null>(null);
+  const [notice,setNotice]=useState('');
+  const [busy,setBusy]=useState(false);
+  const load=useCallback(async()=>{
+    const [p,b]=await Promise.all([api.get<Policy>('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy'),api.get<{batches:Batch[]}>('/agency-revenue/batches')]);
+    setPolicy(p.data);setBatches(b.data.batches);
+  },[]);
+  useEffect(()=>{void load().catch(e=>setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Falha ao carregar lotes'));},[load]);
+  async function act(operation:()=>Promise<unknown>,message:string) {
+    setBusy(true);setError(null);setNotice('');
+    try {await operation();await load();setNotice(message);}
+    catch(e) {setError(e instanceof Error?e.message:'Não foi possível concluir a operação');}
+    finally {setBusy(false);}
+  }
+  return <Card><CardHeader><CardTitle>Lotes de receita</CardTitle></CardHeader><CardContent className="space-y-4">
+    {error&&<p role="alert">{error}</p>}{notice&&<p role="status">{notice}</p>}
+    {policy&&<>
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
+        <label>Recebimento<select className="block rounded border p-2" value={policy.settlementMode} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,settlementMode:e.target.value as Policy['settlementMode']})}><option value="PER_SALE">Por venda</option><option value="BATCH_WINDOW">Lotes por janela mensal</option></select></label>
+        {policy.settlementMode==='BATCH_WINDOW'&&<>
+          <label>Dia de fechamento<input className="block w-28 rounded border p-2" type="number" min="1" max="31" value={policy.closingDay??''} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,closingDay:e.target.value===''?null:Number(e.target.value)})}/></label>
+          <label>Dias até pagamento<input className="block w-28 rounded border p-2" type="number" min="0" max="365" value={policy.paymentDays??''} onChange={e=>setPolicy({...policy,paymentDays:e.target.value===''?null:Number(e.target.value)})}/></label>
+        </>}
+        <button className="rounded border px-3 py-2" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.put('/agency-revenue/settlement-policy',{...policy}),'Política salva.')}>Salvar política de recebimento</button>
+      </div>
+      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Janela: após o fechamento anterior até o dia de fechamento, inclusive. Dias 29–31 usam o último dia disponível do mês. Feche após o término desse dia.</p>
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3"><label>Mês da janela<input className="block rounded border p-2" type="month" value={month} onChange={e=>setMonth(e.target.value)}/></label><button className="rounded border px-3 py-2" disabled={busy||!month||policy.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW'} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.post('/agency-revenue/batches/close',{month}),'Lote fechado.')}>Fechar lote</button></div>
+      <label className="block">Referência do recebimento do lote<input className="block w-full rounded border p-2" maxLength={200} value={reference} onChange={e=>setReference(e.target.value)}/></label>
+      <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-left text-sm"><thead><tr><th>Janela</th><th>Pagamento previsto</th><th>Entradas</th><th>Valor</th><th>Recebimento</th></tr></thead><tbody>{batches.map(b=><tr key={b.id} className="border-t"><td>{b.month}</td><td>{b.expected_payment_date}</td><td>{b.count}</td><td>{formatBRL(b.amount)}</td><td>{b.received_at?'Recebido':<button className="my-2 rounded border p-2" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>void act(()=>api.post(`/agency-revenue/batches/${encodeURIComponent(b.id)}/receive`,{reference}),'Recebimento registrado.')}>Registrar recebimento do lote {b.month}</button>}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
+      {!batches.length&&<p>Nenhum lote fechado.</p>}
+    </>}
+  </CardContent></Card>;
+}

+-- Configured monthly windows; closed batches, membership and receipts are historical.
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_settlement_settings (
+  agency_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  settlement_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (settlement_mode IN ('PER_SALE','BATCH_WINDOW')),
+  closing_day INTEGER CHECK (closing_day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
+  payment_days INTEGER CHECK (payment_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),
+  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  CHECK (settlement_mode='PER_SALE' OR (closing_day IS NOT NULL AND payment_days IS NOT NULL)),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,updated_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batches (
+  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  window_month DATE NOT NULL CHECK (extract(day FROM window_month)=1),
+  window_start DATE NOT NULL,
+  window_end DATE NOT NULL CHECK (window_end>window_start),
+  expected_payment_date DATE NOT NULL CHECK (expected_payment_date>=window_end),
+  policy_snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,id),
+  UNIQUE (agency_id,window_month),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_entries (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  amount NUMERIC(14,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount>=0),
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,entry_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,entry_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_entries(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_batch_receipts (
+  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+  reference TEXT CHECK (length(reference)<=200),
+  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
+  PRIMARY KEY (agency_id,batch_id),
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,batch_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_batches(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
+  FOREIGN KEY (agency_id,created_by) REFERENCES users(agency_id,id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
+);
+ALTER TABLE agency_revenue_entries ADD COLUMN expected_payment_date DATE;
+
+DO $$
+DECLARE t TEXT; r TEXT;
+BEGIN
+  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['agency_revenue_settlement_settings','agency_revenue_batches','agency_revenue_batch_entries','agency_revenue_batch_receipts'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR SELECT USING (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_select_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_insert_tenant',t);
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON %I FROM PUBLIC',t);
+    IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY %I ON %I FOR UPDATE USING (agency_id=current_agency_id()) WITH CHECK (agency_id=current_agency_id())',t||'_update_tenant',t);
+    ELSE
+      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reject_payment_history_change()',t||'_immutable',t);
+    END IF;
+    FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['travel_app_runtime','travel_app_runtime_local'] LOOP
+      IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname=r) THEN
+        EXECUTE format('GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        IF t='agency_revenue_settlement_settings' THEN
+          EXECUTE format('GRANT UPDATE ON %I TO %I',t,r);
+        END IF;
+      END IF;
+    END LOOP;
+  END LOOP;
+END $$;

+import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
+import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
+import { todayCivil } from './report-projections-service';
+
+export interface SettlementPolicy {
+  settlementMode: 'PER_SALE' | 'BATCH_WINDOW';
+  closingDay: number | null;
+  paymentDays: number | null;
+}
+
+export function parseSettlementPolicy(body: unknown): SettlementPolicy {
+  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement policy');
+  const r = body as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (Object.keys(r).some((key) => !['settlementMode','closingDay','paymentDays'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown settlement field');
+  if (r.settlementMode !== 'PER_SALE' && r.settlementMode !== 'BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ValidationError('Invalid settlement mode');
+  if (r.settlementMode === 'PER_SALE') return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: null, paymentDays: null };
+  if (!Number.isInteger(r.closingDay) || Number(r.closingDay)<1 || Number(r.closingDay)>31 ||
+      !Number.isInteger(r.paymentDays) || Number(r.paymentDays)<0 || Number(r.paymentDays)>365) throw new ValidationError('Closing day 1–31 and payment days 0–365 are required');
+  return { settlementMode: r.settlementMode, closingDay: Number(r.closingDay), paymentDays: Number(r.paymentDays) };
+}
+
+/** Window is (previous closing date, current closing date], using civil dates. */
+export function settlementWindow(month: string, day: number, paymentDays: number) {
+  if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(month) || month<'1900-01' || month>'9998-12') throw new ValidationError('Month must be YYYY-MM');
+  const year=Number(month.slice(0,4)), m=Number(month.slice(5));
+  const cutoff=(offset:number) => new Date(Date.UTC(year,m-1+offset,Math.min(day,new Date(Date.UTC(year,m+offset,0)).getUTCDate())));
+  const start=cutoff(-1), end=cutoff(0);
+  const expected=new Date(end.getTime()+paymentDays*86400000);
+  return { start:start.toISOString().slice(0,10), end:end.toISOString().slice(0,10), expected:expected.toISOString().slice(0,10) };
+}
+
+async function policy(client: TenantTransactionClient): Promise<SettlementPolicy> {
+  const result=await client.query<SettlementPolicy>(`SELECT settlement_mode AS "settlementMode",closing_day AS "closingDay",payment_days AS "paymentDays"
+    FROM agency_revenue_settlement_settings WHERE agency_id=$1`,[getAgencyId()]);
+  return result.rows[0] ?? {settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null};
+}
+
+export async function getSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(policy);
+}
+
+export async function saveSettlementPolicy(database: DatabaseRuntime,input:SettlementPolicy) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${getAgencyId()}`]);
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_settlement_settings (agency_id,settlement_mode,closing_day,payment_days,updated_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT (agency_id) DO UPDATE SET settlement_mode=$2,closing_day=$3,payment_days=$4,updated_by=$5,updated_at=now()`,
+    [getAgencyId(),input.settlementMode,input.closingDay,input.paymentDays,getUserId()]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_SETTLEMENT_CONFIGURED,entityType:'agency_revenue_settlement',required:true,metadata:{toStatus:input.settlementMode}});
+    return input;
+  });
+}
+
+export async function listRevenueBatches(database: DatabaseRuntime) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => (await client.query(`SELECT b.id,to_char(b.window_month,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
+    b.window_start::text,b.window_end::text,b.expected_payment_date::text,r.received_at,
+    COALESCE(SUM(e.amount),0)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(e.entry_id)::int AS count
+    FROM agency_revenue_batches b LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries e ON e.agency_id=b.agency_id AND e.batch_id=b.id
+    LEFT JOIN agency_revenue_batch_receipts r ON r.agency_id=b.agency_id AND r.batch_id=b.id
+    WHERE b.agency_id=$1 GROUP BY b.id,r.received_at ORDER BY b.window_month DESC LIMIT 500`,[getAgencyId()])).rows);
+}
+
+export async function closeRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,month:string) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  settlementWindow(month,1,0);
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    const existing=await client.query<{id:string}>('SELECT id FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND window_month=$2::date',[agency,month+'-01']);
+    if(existing.rows[0]) return {id:existing.rows[0].id};
+    const config=await policy(client);
+    if(config.settlementMode!=='BATCH_WINDOW') throw new ConflictError('Configure BATCH_WINDOW before closing a batch');
+    const window=settlementWindow(month,config.closingDay!,config.paymentDays!);
+    if(window.end>=todayCivil()) throw new ConflictError('The closing civil day must have ended');
+    // Match the sale -> entry lock order of payment/cancellation paths. Recheck entries after locking sales.
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{id:string}>(`SELECT e.id FROM agency_revenue_entries e WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.status='ENTITLED'
+      AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date>$2::date AND (e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date<=$3::date
+      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id)
+      ORDER BY e.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,window.start,window.end]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length) throw new ConflictError('No entitled revenue in this window');
+    const batch=await client.query<{id:string}>(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batches (agency_id,window_month,window_start,window_end,expected_payment_date,policy_snapshot,created_by)
+      VALUES ($1,$2::date,$3::date,$4::date,$5::date,$6::jsonb,$7) RETURNING id`,[agency,month+'-01',window.start,window.end,window.expected,JSON.stringify(config),getUserId()]);
+    const id=batch.rows[0]!.id;
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_entries (agency_id,batch_id,entry_id,amount)
+      SELECT agency_id,$2,id,amount FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($3::text[])`,[agency,id,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id)]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET status='AVAILABLE',expected_payment_date=$3::date,updated_at=now()
+      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=ANY($2::text[])`,[agency,entries.rows.map(e=>e.id),window.expected]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_CLOSED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'AVAILABLE'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}
+
+export async function receiveRevenueBatch(database:DatabaseRuntime,id:string,reference:string|null) {
+  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
+  if(reference!==null && (typeof reference!=='string'||reference.length>200)) throw new ValidationError('Invalid reference');
+  return database.withTenantTransaction(async client => {
+    const agency=getAgencyId();
+    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))',[`revenue-batches:${agency}`]);
+    if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batches WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
+    if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_batch_receipts WHERE agency_id=$1 AND batch_id=$2',[agency,id])).rowCount) return {id};
+    await client.query(`SELECT s.id FROM sales s WHERE s.agency_id=$1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries e
+      JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id
+      WHERE e.agency_id=s.agency_id AND e.sale_id=s.id AND be.batch_id=$2) ORDER BY s.id FOR UPDATE`,[agency,id]);
+    const entries=await client.query<{status:string}>(`SELECT e.status FROM agency_revenue_entries e JOIN agency_revenue_batch_entries be
+      ON be.agency_id=e.agency_id AND be.entry_id=e.id WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 FOR UPDATE OF e`,[agency,id]);
+    if(!entries.rows.length||entries.rows.some(e=>e.status!=='AVAILABLE')) throw new ConflictError('Every batch entry must be AVAILABLE');
+    await client.query(`INSERT INTO agency_revenue_batch_receipts (agency_id,batch_id,reference,created_by) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)`,[agency,id,reference,getUserId()]);
+    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries e SET status='RECEIVED',received_amount=be.amount,received_at=now(),received_reference=$3,updated_at=now()
+      FROM agency_revenue_batch_entries be WHERE be.agency_id=$1 AND be.batch_id=$2 AND e.agency_id=be.agency_id AND e.id=be.entry_id`,[agency,id,reference]);
+    await recordAuditEvent(client,{eventType:AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_BATCH_RECEIVED,entityType:'agency_revenue_batch',entityId:id,required:true,metadata:{toStatus:'RECEIVED'}});
+    return {id};
+  });
+}

+import type {FormSnapshot} from './sale-form-fields';
+
+type ImportPendingField = 'category'|'supplier'|'destination';
+
+export function buildImportFormSnapshot(pending: readonly ImportPendingField[]): FormSnapshot {
+  return {
+    categoryId:'',categoryName:'Importação histórica',formRevision:0,capturedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
+    importPending:[...pending],
+    // Destination remains editable through the existing typed form endpoint.
+    fields:[{fieldKey:'destination_text',label:'Destino',enabled:true,required:false}],
+  };
+}
+
+/** Requirements are historical, their missing state is always derived from the live sale. */
+export function remainingImportFields(snapshot:FormSnapshot|null,categoryId:string|null,destination:string|null,hasSupplier:boolean):string[] {
+  return (snapshot?.importPending??[]).flatMap(field=>{
+    if(field==='category'&&!categoryId) return ['category_id'];
+    if(field==='destination'&&!destination?.trim()) return ['destination_text'];
+    if(field==='supplier'&&!hasSupplier) return ['supplier_id'];
+    return [];
+  });
+}

new file mode 100644

+import { describe,expect,it,vi } from 'vitest';
+import { runWithTenantContext } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
+import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
+import { closeRevenueBatch,parseSettlementPolicy,settlementWindow,receiveRevenueBatch } from '../src/agency-revenue-batches';
+import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../src/database';
+
+describe('revenue settlement windows',()=>{
+  it('clamps monthly closing days and adds real calendar days including leap years',()=>{
+    expect(settlementWindow('2024-02',31,5)).toEqual({start:'2024-01-31',end:'2024-02-29',expected:'2024-03-05'});
+    expect(settlementWindow('2026-01',15,20)).toEqual({start:'2025-12-15',end:'2026-01-15',expected:'2026-02-04'});
+  });
+  it.each(['2026-00','2026-13','x','0000-01'])('rejects invalid month %s',month=>expect(()=>settlementWindow(month,15,1)).toThrow());
+  it('requires an explicit calendar only for batch settlement',()=>{
+    expect(parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE'})).toEqual({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',closingDay:null,paymentDays:null});
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW'})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'BATCH_WINDOW',closingDay:31,paymentDays:-1})).toThrow();
+    expect(()=>parseSettlementPolicy({settlementMode:'PER_SALE',agencyId:'other'})).toThrow();
+  });
+  it('refuses writes for managers before opening a transaction',async()=>{
+    const withTenantTransaction=vi.fn();
+    const database={withTenantTransaction} as unknown as DatabaseRuntime;
+    await runWithTenantContext({agencyId:'a',userId:'u',userRole:UserRole.MANAGER,email:'u@example.test'},async()=>{
+      await expect(closeRevenueBatch(database,'2026-01')).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+      await expect(receiveRevenueBatch(database,'batch',null)).rejects.toMatchObject({code:'FORBIDDEN'});
+    });
+    expect(withTenantTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

-- ============================================================
-- AGENCY REVENUE (ADR-007, Frente 6/7 -- Onda D)
-- ============================================================
-- Receita da agência: o que o fornecedor deve à AGÊNCIA. É outra grandeza
-- que valor vendido, repasse ao fornecedor e comissão de funcionário; não
-- compartilha tabela com nenhuma delas.
--   * agency_revenue_scopes         escopo (fornecedor/produto/categoria);
--   * agency_revenue_rule_versions  versões append-only da regra;
--   * agency_revenue_entries        uma linha por item de venda, com snapshot
--                                    do cálculo e estados
--                                    PENDING_RULE|FORECAST|ENTITLED|AVAILABLE|
--                                    RECEIVED|CANCELLED.
-- Lotes/janelas (D7) pertencem à Onda E: AVAILABLE existe como estado, mas
-- nada o produz ainda. Aditiva; não altera dados existentes.
-- commission_entries.agency_revenue_entry_id: vínculo OPCIONAL, só para
-- análise (D3: sem netting, sem dependência do recebimento do fornecedor).
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_scopes (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::TEXT,
  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES agencies (id),
  supplier_id TEXT,
  product_id TEXT,
  category_id TEXT,
  created_by_user_id TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  legacy_ref TEXT,
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_scopes_agency_id_key UNIQUE (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_scopes_supplier_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, supplier_id) REFERENCES suppliers (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_scopes_product_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, product_id) REFERENCES travel_products (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_scopes_category_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, category_id) REFERENCES commission_categories (agency_id, id)
);
-- One scope per combination; NULL parts are normalised so the default scope is unique too.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX agency_revenue_scopes_tuple_uidx ON agency_revenue_scopes (
  agency_id, COALESCE(supplier_id, ''), COALESCE(product_id, ''), COALESCE(category_id, '')
);

CREATE TABLE agency_revenue_rule_versions (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::TEXT,
  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  version_number INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (version_number >= 1),
  effective_from DATE NOT NULL,
  mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('PERCENTAGE', 'FIXED_PER_PASSENGER', 'FIXED_PER_SALE', 'FIXED_PER_ITEM', 'NONE')),
  base_selector TEXT CHECK (base_selector IN ('LINE_TOTAL', 'NET_FARE', 'TAXES', 'FEES', 'TAXES_AND_FEES', 'MARGIN')),
  value NUMERIC(14, 4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (value >= 0),
  entitlement_event TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ON_FULL_SETTLEMENT'
    CHECK (entitlement_event IN ('ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION', 'ON_FULL_SETTLEMENT', 'ON_BALANCE_DEADLINE')),
  collection_model TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'AGENCY_COLLECTS'
    CHECK (collection_model IN ('AGENCY_COLLECTS', 'SUPPLIER_COLLECTS')),
  reason TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 300),
  created_by_user_id TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  calc_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_agency_id_key UNIQUE (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_scope_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, scope_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_scopes (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_version_uq UNIQUE (agency_id, scope_id, version_number),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_base_check
    CHECK ((mode = 'PERCENTAGE') = (base_selector IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_percent_check
    CHECK (mode <> 'PERCENTAGE' OR value <= 100),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_margin_check
    CHECK (base_selector IS DISTINCT FROM 'MARGIN' OR collection_model = 'AGENCY_COLLECTS'),
  -- Supplier-collected sales have no customer settlement to wait for.
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_rule_versions_supplier_collects_check
    CHECK (collection_model = 'AGENCY_COLLECTS' OR entitlement_event = 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION')
);

  agency_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  sale_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  sale_item_id TEXT NOT NULL,

  entitlement_event TEXT,
  amount NUMERIC(14, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (amount >= 0),
  base_amount NUMERIC(14, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (base_amount >= 0),
  calc_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  memory JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  entitled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  received_amount NUMERIC(14, 2),
  received_reference TEXT,
  cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_by_user_id TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  legacy_ref TEXT,
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_agency_id_key UNIQUE (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_sale_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, sale_id) REFERENCES sales (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_item_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, sale_item_id) REFERENCES sale_items (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_supplier_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, supplier_id) REFERENCES suppliers (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_rule_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, rule_version_id) REFERENCES agency_revenue_rule_versions (agency_id, id),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_pending_zero_check
    CHECK (status <> 'PENDING_RULE' OR amount = 0),
  CONSTRAINT agency_revenue_entries_received_check
    CHECK ((status = 'RECEIVED') = (received_at IS NOT NULL))
);
-- At most one live entry per item (cancelled history is kept).

/**
 * Commission release by receipt (Onda E2: COM-03 + Frente 8).
 *
 * The payment METHOD says whether a sale needs its money received (payment_method_receipt_rules,
 * tri-state: no row = never decided); the agency's FINANCIAL PROFILE says how that affects release
 * (commission_financial_profiles, append-only with effective date). Missing configuration blocks the
 * feature with a 409 naming what is missing; it is never turned into "release on sale".
 * Entries created before migration 108 are LEGACY and keep their current behaviour (no reprocessing).
 * Pure rules live in commission-release.ts; this file only reads the real receipts (Onda C) and payments.
 */
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ReleasePolicyMissingError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
import {
  allocableAmount,
  entryStatusAfterPayments,
  releasedAmount,
  resolveReleasePolicy,
  type ReleaseEffect,
  type ReleasePolicy,
} from './commission-release';
import { getAgencyId, getUserId } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';

export interface EntryRelease {
  entryId: string;
  recognized: number;
  received: number;
  saleTotal: number;
  released: number;
  paid: number;
  allocable: number;
  policy: ReleasePolicy;
  status: 'PAID' | 'PAYABLE' | 'APPROVED';
}

const money = (value: string | null | undefined): number => Math.round(Number(value ?? 0) * 100) / 100;

export const lockEntrySettlement = (client: TenantTransactionClient, agencyId: string, entryId: string) =>
  client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended($1,0))', [`commission-settle:${agencyId}:${entryId}`]);

export async function loadActiveFinancialProfile(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  agencyId: string,
): Promise<{ profileId: string; effect: ReleaseEffect } | null> {
  const result = await client.query<{ id: string; effect: ReleaseEffect }>(
    `SELECT id, effect FROM commission_financial_profiles
     WHERE agency_id = $1 AND effective_from <= (now() AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date
     ORDER BY effective_from DESC, created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`,
    [agencyId],
  );
  const row = result.rows[0];
  return row ? { profileId: row.id, effect: row.effect } : null;
}

/** Tri-state for the sale: undecided if any method has no rule; required if any decided method requires it. */
export function receiptRequirement(methods: string[], rules: Map<string, boolean>): boolean | null {
  if (methods.length === 0) return null;
  let required = false;
  for (const method of methods) {
    const rule = rules.get(method);
    if (rule === undefined) return null;
    if (rule) required = true;
  }
  return required;
}

async function resolvePolicyForMethods(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  agencyId: string,
  methods: string[],
): Promise<ReleasePolicy> {
  const rulesResult = await client.query<{ method: string; requires_receipt: boolean }>(
    `SELECT method, requires_receipt FROM payment_method_receipt_rules WHERE agency_id = $1 AND method = ANY($2::text[])`,
    [agencyId, methods],
  );
  const profile = await loadActiveFinancialProfile(client, agencyId);
  return resolveReleasePolicy({
    hasPaymentMethod: methods.length > 0,
    requiresReceipt: receiptRequirement(methods, new Map(rulesResult.rows.map((row) => [row.method, row.requires_receipt]))),
    profile,
  });
}

/** Capture policy at obligation creation; later agency configuration cannot rewrite it. */
export async function captureSaleReleasePolicySnapshot(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  agencyId: string,
  saleId: string,
): Promise<ReleasePolicy> {
  const sale = await client.query<{ methods: string[] | null }>(
    `SELECT ARRAY(SELECT jsonb_array_elements_text(payment_condition_snapshot->'condition'->'paymentMethods')) AS methods
       FROM sales WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2`, [agencyId, saleId],
  );
  if (!sale.rows[0]) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
  return resolvePolicyForMethods(client, agencyId, sale.rows[0].methods ?? []);
}

/** Computes the release state of one entry. Caller should hold the settlement lock when it will act on it. */
export async function computeEntryRelease(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  agencyId: string,
  entryId: string,
): Promise<EntryRelease> {
  const entryResult = await client.query<{
    sale_id: string | null; amount: string; release_mode: string; release_policy_snapshot: ReleasePolicy | null;
    payroll_entry_id: string | null; status: string;
  }>(
    `SELECT sale_id, amount::text, release_mode, release_policy_snapshot, payroll_entry_id, status FROM commission_entries
     WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`,
    [agencyId, entryId],
  );
  const entry = entryResult.rows[0];
  if (!entry) throw new NotFoundError('Commission entry not found');
  const recognized = money(entry.amount);

  const paidResult = await client.query<{ paid: string }>(
    `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pa.amount), 0)::text AS paid
     FROM payables p JOIN payment_allocations pa ON pa.agency_id = p.agency_id AND pa.payable_id = p.id
     WHERE p.agency_id = $1 AND p.commission_entry_id = $2 AND p.status <> 'CANCELLED'`,
    [agencyId, entryId],
  );
  // payroll channel: the payroll pays as a whole, so its commissions count as paid only when the entry is PAID
  const paid = money(paidResult.rows[0]?.paid) + (entry.payroll_entry_id !== null && entry.status === 'PAID' ? recognized : 0);

  const withState = (policy: ReleasePolicy, received: number, saleTotal: number): EntryRelease => {
    const released = releasedAmount(policy, recognized, received, saleTotal);
    const channel = entry.payroll_entry_id !== null ? 'PAYROLL' : 'PAYABLE';
    return {
      entryId, recognized, received, saleTotal, released, paid,
      allocable: allocableAmount(released, paid), policy,
/**
 * Sales -- CRUD for sales with customer join, plus confirm/cancel/mark-paid.

import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type { preHandlerHookHandler } from 'fastify';
import { requireRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/types';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../database';
import { NotFoundError } from '../errors';
import {
  cancelSale,
  confirmSale,
  createSale,
  getSaleWithCustomerById,
  listSalesWithCustomer,
  markSalePaid,
  updateSale,
} from '../sales';
import {
  parseCreateSaleInput,
  parseUpdateSaleInput,
} from '../commercial-input-parsing';

export interface SalesRoutesOptions {
  database: DatabaseRuntime;
  protectedHooks: preHandlerHookHandler[];
}

export function registerSalesRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: SalesRoutesOptions,
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get('/sales', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.VIEWER);
    const all = await listSalesWithCustomer(database);
    const incomplete = (request.query as Record<string, string | undefined>).incomplete === 'true';
    const sales = incomplete ? all.filter((sale) => (sale.missingRequiredFields ?? []).length > 0) : all;
    return { sales };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>(
    '/sales/:id',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.VIEWER);
      const sale = await getSaleWithCustomerById(database, request.params.id);

      if (!sale) {
        throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
      }

      return { sale };
    }
  );

  app.post('/sales', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.AGENT);
    const data = parseCreateSaleInput(request.body);
    const sale = await createSale(database, data);

    reply.code(201);
    return { sale };
  });

  app.patch<{ Params: { id: string } }>(
    '/sales/:id',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.AGENT);
      const data = parseUpdateSaleInput(request.body);
      const sale = await updateSale(database, request.params.id, data);

      if (!sale) {
        throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
      }

      return { sale };
    }
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>(
    '/sales/:id/confirm',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
      const sale = await confirmSale(database, request.params.id);
      if (!sale) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
      return { sale };
    }
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>(
    '/sales/:id/cancel',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
      const sale = await cancelSale(database, request.params.id);
      if (!sale) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
      return { sale };
    }
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>(
    '/sales/:id/mark-paid',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
      const sale = await markSalePaid(database, request.params.id);
      if (!sale) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
      return { sale };
    }
  );

  import {

>   parseUpdateSaleInput,
> } from '../commercial-input-parsing';

/** Loads a category and its stored profile rules inside an open tenant transaction. */
export async function loadCategoryWithRules(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  agencyId: string,
  categoryId: string,
  lock: false | 'update' | 'share' = false,
): Promise<{ category: SaleCategory; rules: SaleFormRule[]; sortOrders: Map<string, number> } | null> {
  // 'update' is taken by the profile editor / category edits, 'share' by sales that snapshot the
  // profile: a sale then sees one consistent revision and cannot race a replace or a deactivation.
  const lockClause = lock === 'update' ? ' FOR UPDATE' : lock === 'share' ? ' FOR SHARE' : '';
  const categoryResult = await client.query<CategoryRow>(
    `SELECT ${CATEGORY_COLUMNS} FROM commission_categories WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2${lockClause}`,
    [agencyId, categoryId],
  );
  const row = categoryResult.rows[0];
  if (!row) return null;
  const ruleResult = await client.query<RuleRow>(
    `SELECT field_key, enabled, required, sort_order FROM sale_category_fields
      WHERE agency_id = $1 AND category_id = $2 ORDER BY sort_order, field_key`,
    [agencyId, categoryId],
  );
  return {
    category: toCategory(row),
    rules: ruleResult.rows.map((r) => ({ fieldKey: r.field_key, enabled: r.enabled, required: r.required })),
    sortOrders: new Map(ruleResult.rows.map((r) => [r.field_key, r.sort_order])),
  };
}

function mergeForm(
  category: SaleCategory,
  rules: readonly SaleFormRule[],
  sortOrders: ReadonlyMap<string, number>,

    return {
      fieldKey: field.key,
      storage: field.storage,
      label: field.label,
      type: field.type,
      group: field.group,
      locked: field.locked,
      enabled: field.locked ? true : (rule?.enabled ?? false),
      required: field.locked ? false : (rule?.required ?? false),
      sortOrder: sortOrders.get(field.key) ?? index,

/**
 * Sale items ("Turbine sua Viagem" line items) (Agent 08 -- Upsell).
 *
 * Read floor mirrors GET /sales (VIEWER+, tenant-wide list, no narrower
 * client-owned subset to withhold). Create/cancel mirror the write floors
 * used elsewhere on Sale: creating a line item changes the Sale's
 * authoritative total (AGENT+, same floor as PATCH /sales/:id which also
 * changes `amount`); cancelling reverses that total (MANAGER+, same floor
 * as POST /sales/:id/cancel).
 *
 * Registered as one unit from app.ts, same convention as routes/sales.ts.
 */

import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type { preHandlerHookHandler } from 'fastify';
import { requireRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/types';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../database';
import { NotFoundError } from '../errors';
import {
  cancelSaleItem,
  createSaleItem,
  listSaleItems,
  parseCreateSaleItemInput,
} from '../sale-items';

export interface SaleItemsRoutesOptions {
  database: DatabaseRuntime;
  protectedHooks: preHandlerHookHandler[];
}

export function registerSaleItemsRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: SaleItemsRoutesOptions,
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get<{ Params: { saleId: string } }>(
    '/sales/:saleId/items',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.VIEWER);
      const items = await listSaleItems(database, request.params.saleId);
      return { saleItems: items };
    }
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { saleId: string } }>(
    '/sales/:saleId/items',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request, reply) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.AGENT);
      const data = parseCreateSaleItemInput(request.params.saleId, request.body);
      const saleItem = await createSaleItem(database, data);

      reply.code(201);
      return { saleItem };
    }
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>(
    '/sale-items/:id/cancel',
    { preHandler: protectedHooks },
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
      const saleItem = await cancelSaleItem(database, request.params.id);
      if (!saleItem) throw new NotFoundError('Sale item not found');
      return { saleItem };
    }
  );
}
